     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const { fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes } = require("./drawRepository");
     4: const { writeStatisticsSnapshot } = require("./statisticsEngine");
     5: 
     6: // Ajuste estes requires caso os arquivos estejam em outro caminho
     7: const { buildRanking } = require("../../src/utils/buildRanking");
     8: const { applyScoreEngine } = require("../../src/utils/scoreEngine");
     9: 
    10: async function bootstrapSnapshot(lottery = "PT_RIO") {
    11: 
    12:   const draws = await fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes({
    13:     lottery,
    14:     pageSize: 500,
    15:   });
    16: 
    17:   console.log("Draws carregados:", draws.length);
    18: 
    19:   const built = buildRanking(draws);
    20: 
    21:   const ranking = Array.isArray(built?.ranking)
    22:     ? built.ranking
    23:     : [];
    24: 
    25:   const rankingScored = applyScoreEngine(ranking);
    26: 
    27:   await writeStatisticsSnapshot({
    28:     uf: lottery,
    29:     scope: "dashboard_full",
    30:     data: {
    31:       version: 1,
    32:       totalDraws: built.totalDraws,
    33:       uniqueDays: built.uniqueDays,
    34:       totalOcorrencias: built.totalOcorrencias,
    35:       countMode: built.countMode,
    36:       ranking,
    37:       rankingScored,
    38:       top3: rankingScored.slice(0,3),
    39:       integrityIssues: built.integrityIssues || [],
    40:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    41:     },
    42:   });
    43: 
    44:   return {
    45:     draws: draws.length,
    46:     ranking: ranking.length,
    47:     top3: rankingScored.slice(0,3),
    48:   };
    49: }
    50: 
    51: module.exports = {
    52:   bootstrapSnapshot,
    53: };

     1: const { computeBounds } = require("../routes/bounds");
     2: const {
     3:   readStatisticsSnapshot,
     4:   writeStatisticsSnapshot,
     5:   getAdminDb,
     6: } = require("./statisticsEngine");
     7: 
     8: const {
     9:   readSnapshotMetadata,
    10:   writeSnapshotMetadata,
    11: } = require("./snapshotMetadata");
    12: 
    13: 
    14: async function fetchNewDrawsAfter(db, lottery, lastProcessedDrawId, limit = 25) {
    15:   if (!lastProcessedDrawId) {
    16:     return {
    17:       requiresBootstrap: true,
    18:       draws: [],
    19:     };
    20:   }
    21: 
    22:   const admin = require("firebase-admin");
    23:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
    24: 
    25:   const snap = await db
    26:     .collection("draws")
    27:     .where("lottery_key", "==", String(lottery).toUpperCase())
    28:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
    29:     .startAfter(String(lastProcessedDrawId))
    30:     .limit(limit)
    31:     .get();
    32: 
    33:   return {
    34:     requiresBootstrap: false,
    35:     draws: snap.docs.map((doc) => ({
    36:       id: doc.id,
    37:       ...doc.data(),
    38:     })),
    39:   };
    40: }
    41: 
    42: async function updateSnapshot({
    43:   lottery = "PT_RIO",
    44:   force = false,
    45: } = {}) {
    46: 
    47:   const db = getAdminDb();
    48: 
    49:   // Bounds atuais do Firestore
    50:   const { result: bounds } = await computeBounds(db, lottery);
    51: 
    52:   // Snapshot existente
    53:   const current = await readStatisticsSnapshot({
    54:     uf: lottery,
    55:     scope: "dashboard_full",
    56:   });
    57: 
    58:   // Metadata de controle incremental
    59:   const metadata = await readSnapshotMetadata(lottery);
    60: 
    61:   const lastProcessedDrawId = metadata?.lastProcessedDrawId || null;
    62:   const latestDrawId = bounds?.maxDocId || null;
    63: 
    64:   const upToDate =
    65:     !force &&
    66:     lastProcessedDrawId &&
    67:     latestDrawId &&
    68:     String(lastProcessedDrawId) === String(latestDrawId);
    69: 
    70:   if (upToDate) {
    71:     return {
    72:       ok: true,
    73:       lottery,
    74:       mode: "noop",
    75:       reason: "snapshot_already_up_to_date",
    76:       bounds,
    77:       metadata,
    78:       processed: 0,
    79:     };
    80:   }
    81: 
    82:   const pending = await fetchNewDrawsAfter(
    83:     db,
    84:     lottery,
    85:     lastProcessedDrawId,
    86:     25
    87:   );
    88: 
    89:   await writeSnapshotMetadata(lottery, {
    90:     lastKnownMinYmd: bounds?.minYmd || null,
    91:     lastKnownMaxYmd: bounds?.maxYmd || null,
    92:     latestDrawId,
    93:     lastCheckAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    94:     pendingIncrementalEngine: true,
    95:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
    96:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
    97:   });
    98: 
    99:   return {
   100:     ok: true,
   101:     lottery,
   102:     mode: pending.requiresBootstrap
   103:       ? "bootstrap_required"
   104:       : force
   105:       ? "force_pending"
   106:       : "incremental_pending",
   107:     bounds,
   108:     snapshot: current.data || null,
   109:     metadata,
   110:     pendingNewDraws: pending.draws,
   111:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
   112:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
   113:     processed: 0,
   114:     nextStep: pending.requiresBootstrap
   115:       ? "IMPLEMENT_BOOTSTRAP_SNAPSHOT"
   116:       : "IMPLEMENT_APPLY_NEW_DRAWS",
   117:   };
   118: }
   119: 
   120: module.exports = {
   121:   updateSnapshot,
   122: };

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const {
     4:   getDb,
     5: } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
     6: 
     7: const {
     8:   readMetadata,
     9:   writeMetadata,
    10:   upsertHistoryMonth,
    11:   listHistoryMonths,
    12:   deduplicateDraws,
    13: } = require("./top3HistoryRepository");
    14: 
    15: function safeArray(value) {
    16:   return Array.isArray(value)
    17:     ? value
    18:     : [];
    19: }
    20: 
    21: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    22:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
    23:     .trim()
    24:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    25: }
    26: 
    27: function validYmd(value) {
    28:   const ymd = String(value || "").trim();
    29: 
    30:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(ymd)
    31:     ? ymd
    32:     : null;
    33: }
    34: 
    35: function resolveDb(dependencies = {}) {
    36:   return dependencies.db || getDb();
    37: }
    38: 
    39: async function readDrawWithPrizes(
    40:   database,
    41:   drawDoc
    42: ) {
    43:   if (!drawDoc || !drawDoc.exists) {
    44:     return null;
    45:   }
    46: 
    47:   const data = drawDoc.data() || {};
    48: 
    49:   const prizesSnap = await drawDoc.ref
    50:     .collection("prizes")
    51:     .get();
    52: 
    53:   const prizes = prizesSnap.docs.map(
    54:     (doc) => ({
    55:       id: doc.id,
    56:       ...(doc.data() || {}),
    57:     })
    58:   );
    59: 
    60:   return {
    61:     id: drawDoc.id,
    62:     drawId: drawDoc.id,
    63:     ...data,
    64:     prizes,
    65:   };
    66: }
    67: 
    68: async function loadImportedDraws(
    69:   importResult = {},
    70:   dependencies = {}
    71: ) {
    72:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
    73: 
    74:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
    75:     importResult.lotteryKey
    76:   );
    77: 
    78:   const date = validYmd(
    79:     importResult.date
    80:   );
    81: 
    82:   const targetDrawIds = safeArray(
    83:     importResult.targetDrawIds
    84:   )
    85:     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim())
    86:     .filter(Boolean);
    87: 
    88:   const docs = [];
    89: 
    90:   if (targetDrawIds.length) {
    91:     for (const drawId of targetDrawIds) {
    92:       const snap = await database
    93:         .collection("draws")
    94:         .doc(drawId)
    95:         .get();
    96: 
    97:       if (snap.exists) {
    98:         docs.push(snap);
    99:       }
   100:     }
   101:   } else if (date) {
   102:     const snap = await database
   103:       .collection("draws")
   104:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey)
   105:       .where("ymd", "==", date)
   106:       .get();
   107: 
   108:     docs.push(...snap.docs);
   109:   }
   110: 
   111:   const draws = [];
   112: 
   113:   for (const doc of docs) {
   114:     const draw = await readDrawWithPrizes(
   115:       database,
   116:       doc
   117:     );
   118: 
   119:     if (!draw) {
   120:       continue;
   121:     }
   122: 
   123:     const drawLotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   124:       draw.lottery_key ||
   125:       draw.lotteryKey
   126:     );
   127: 
   128:     if (drawLotteryKey !== lotteryKey) {
   129:       continue;
   130:     }
   131: 
   132:     if (
   133:       date &&
   134:       String(draw.ymd || draw.date || "") !== date
   135:     ) {
   136:       continue;
   137:     }
   138: 
   139:     draws.push(draw);
   140:   }
   141: 
   142:   return deduplicateDraws(draws);
   143: }
   144: 
   145: function summarizeMonths(months = []) {
   146:   const ordered = [...safeArray(months)]
   147:     .sort((a, b) =>
   148:       String(a.yearMonth || a.id)
   149:         .localeCompare(
   150:           String(b.yearMonth || b.id)
   151:         )
   152:     );
   153: 
   154:   const allDraws = deduplicateDraws(
   155:     ordered.flatMap(
   156:       (month) =>
   157:         safeArray(month.draws)
   158:     )
   159:   );
   160: 
   161:   const first = allDraws[0] || null;
   162:   const last =
   163:     allDraws[allDraws.length - 1] || null;
   164: 
   165:   return {
   166:     totalDraws: allDraws.length,
   167:     monthCount: ordered.length,
   168:     months: ordered.map(
   169:       (month) =>
   170:         month.yearMonth || month.id
   171:     ),
   172:     firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
   173:     lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
   174:     firstDrawId:
   175:       first?.drawId || null,
   176:     lastDrawId:
   177:       last?.drawId || null,
   178:   };
   179: }
   180: 
   181: async function markHistoryStale(
   182:   lotteryKey,
   183:   error,
   184:   dependencies = {}
   185: ) {
   186:   const saveMetadata =
   187:     dependencies.writeMetadata ||
   188:     writeMetadata;
   189: 
   190:   try {
   191:     await saveMetadata(
   192:       lotteryKey,
   193:       {
   194:         bootstrapStatus: "stale",
   195:         staleReason:
   196:           String(
   197:             error?.message ||
   198:             error ||
   199:             "incremental_sync_failed"
   200:           ),
   201:         staleAt:
   202:           new Date().toISOString(),
   203:       },
   204:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   205:     );
   206: 
   207:     return true;
   208:   } catch (metadataError) {
   209:     console.error(
   210:       "[TOP3-HISTORY] Falha também ao marcar snapshot como stale:",
   211:       metadataError?.message ||
   212:       metadataError
   213:     );
   214: 
   215:     return false;
   216:   }
   217: }
   218: 
   219: async function syncImportedResultToTop3History(
   220:   importResult = {},
   221:   dependencies = {}
   222: ) {
   223:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   224:     importResult.lotteryKey
   225:   );
   226: 
   227:   const loadMetadata =
   228:     dependencies.readMetadata ||
   229:     readMetadata;
   230: 
   231:   const saveMonth =
   232:     dependencies.upsertHistoryMonth ||
   233:     upsertHistoryMonth;
   234: 
   235:   const loadMonths =
   236:     dependencies.listHistoryMonths ||
   237:     listHistoryMonths;
   238: 
   239:   const saveMetadata =
   240:     dependencies.writeMetadata ||
   241:     writeMetadata;
   242: 
   243:   try {
   244:     if (
   245:       importResult.blocked === true ||
   246:       !validYmd(importResult.date)
   247:     ) {
   248:       return {
   249:         ok: true,
   250:         skipped: true,
   251:         reason:
   252:           importResult.blocked === true
   253:             ? "import_blocked"
   254:             : "invalid_date",
   255:       };
   256:     }
   257: 
   258:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
   259:       lotteryKey,
   260:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   261:     );
   262: 
   263:     const metadata =
   264:       metadataResult?.data || null;
   265: 
   266:     if (
   267:       metadataResult?.exists !== true ||
   268:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus !== "complete"
   269:     ) {
   270:       return {
   271:         ok: true,
   272:         skipped: true,
   273:         reason: "bootstrap_not_complete",
   274:       };
   275:     }
   276: 
   277:     const draws = await (
   278:       dependencies.loadImportedDraws ||
   279:       loadImportedDraws
   280:     )(
   281:       importResult,
   282:       dependencies
   283:     );
   284: 
   285:     if (!draws.length) {
   286:       return {
   287:         ok: true,
   288:         skipped: true,
   289:         reason: "no_draws_found",
   290:       };
   291:     }
   292: 
   293:     const byMonth = new Map();
   294: 
   295:     for (const draw of draws) {
   296:       const yearMonth = draw.ymd.slice(0, 7);
   297: 
   298:       if (!byMonth.has(yearMonth)) {
   299:         byMonth.set(yearMonth, []);
   300:       }
   301: 
   302:       byMonth.get(yearMonth).push(draw);
   303:     }
   304: 
   305:     const updatedMonths = [];
   306: 
   307:     for (
   308:       const [yearMonth, monthDraws]
   309:       of byMonth.entries()
   310:     ) {
   311:       const payload = await saveMonth(
   312:         lotteryKey,
   313:         yearMonth,
   314:         monthDraws,
   315:         dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   316:       );
   317: 
   318:       updatedMonths.push({
   319:         yearMonth,
   320:         drawCount:
   321:           Number(payload?.drawCount || 0),
   322:       });
   323:     }
   324: 
   325:     const months = await loadMonths(
   326:       lotteryKey,
   327:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   328:     );
   329: 
   330:     const summary = summarizeMonths(months);
   331: 
   332:     await saveMetadata(
   333:       lotteryKey,
   334:       {
   335:         bootstrapStatus: "complete",
   336:         incrementalUpdatedAt:
   337:           new Date().toISOString(),
   338:         totalDraws:
   339:           summary.totalDraws,
   340:         monthCount:
   341:           summary.monthCount,
   342:         months:
   343:           summary.months,
   344:         firstYmd:
   345:           summary.firstYmd,
   346:         lastYmd:
   347:           summary.lastYmd,
   348:         firstDrawId:
   349:           summary.firstDrawId,
   350:         lastDrawId:
   351:           summary.lastDrawId,
   352:         lastProcessedDrawId:
   353:           summary.lastDrawId,
   354:         staleReason: null,
   355:         staleAt: null,
   356:         source:
   357:           "bootstrap_plus_incremental",
   358:       },
   359:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   360:     );
   361: 
   362:     console.log(
   363:       `[TOP3-HISTORY] Incremental OK | ${lotteryKey} | ` +
   364:       `draws=${draws.length} | meses=${updatedMonths
   365:         .map((item) => item.yearMonth)
   366:         .join(",")}`
   367:     );
   368: 
   369:     return {
   370:       ok: true,
   371:       skipped: false,
   372:       lotteryKey,
   373:       importedDraws: draws.length,
   374:       updatedMonths,
   375:       ...summary,
   376:     };
   377:   } catch (error) {
   378:     console.error(
   379:       "[TOP3-HISTORY] Incremental falhou:",
   380:       error?.stack ||
   381:       error?.message ||
   382:       error
   383:     );
   384: 
   385:     const markedStale = await markHistoryStale(
   386:       lotteryKey,
   387:       error,
   388:       dependencies
   389:     );
   390: 
   391:     return {
   392:       ok: false,
   393:       skipped: false,
   394:       lotteryKey,
   395:       markedStale,
   396:       error:
   397:         String(
   398:           error?.message ||
   399:           error ||
   400:           "incremental_sync_failed"
   401:         ),
   402:     };
   403:   }
   404: }
   405: 
   406: module.exports = {
   407:   readDrawWithPrizes,
   408:   loadImportedDraws,
   409:   summarizeMonths,
   410:   markHistoryStale,
   411:   syncImportedResultToTop3History,
   412: };

    74: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    75:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
    76:     .trim()
    77:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    78: }
    79: 
    80: function normalizeYmd(value) {
    81:   const text = String(value || "").trim();
    82: 
    83:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text)) {
    84:     throw new Error(
    85:       "Data inválida. Utilize o formato YYYY-MM-DD."
    86:     );
    87:   }
    88: 
    89:   return text;
    90: }
    91: 
    92: function normalizeHour(value) {
    93:   const text = String(value || "")
    94:     .trim()
    95:     .replace(/[hH]/g, ":");
    96: 
    97:   const match = text.match(
    98:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/
    99:   );
   100: 
   101:   if (!match) {
   102:     throw new Error(
   103:       "Horário inválido. Utilize HH:MM."
   104:     );
   105:   }
   106: 
   107:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
   108:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
   109: 
   110:   if (
   111:     hour < 0 ||
   112:     hour > 23 ||
   113:     minute < 0 ||
   114:     minute > 59
   115:   ) {
   116:     throw new Error("Horário fora do intervalo válido.");
   117:   }
   118: 
   119:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
   120:     minute
   121:   ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   122: }
   123: 
   124: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
   125:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour)}`;
   126: }
   127: 
   128: function subtractDaysYmd(ymd, days) {
   129:   const safeDays = Math.max(
   130:     1,
   131:     Math.min(1460, Number(days || 180))
   132:   );
   133: 
   134:   const date = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00Z`);
   135: 
   136:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
   137:     throw new Error(
   138:       "Não foi possível calcular a janela histórica."
   139:     );
   140:   }
   141: 
   142:   date.setUTCDate(
   143:     date.getUTCDate() - safeDays
   144:   );
   145: 
   146:   return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   147: }
   148: 
   149: function normalizeHistorySource(value) {
   150:   const source = String(value || "auto")
   151:     .trim()
   152:     .toLowerCase();
   153: 
   154:   if (
   155:     source !== "auto" &&
   156:     source !== "snapshot" &&
   157:     source !== "range"
   158:   ) {
   159:     throw new Error(
   160:       "historySource inválido. Utilize auto, snapshot ou range."
   161:     );
   162:   }
   163: 
   164:   return source;
   165: }
   166: 
   167: async function loadPredictionHistory({
   168:   lotteryKey,
   169:   date,
   170:   input = {},
   171:   dependencies = {},
   172: } = {}) {
   173:   const requestedSource = normalizeHistorySource(
   174:     input.historySource
   175:   );
   176: 
   177:   const loadMetadata =
   178:     dependencies.readHistoryMetadata ||
   179:     readMetadata;
   180: 
   181:   const loadFullHistory =
   182:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
   183:     readFullHistory;
   184: 
   185:   const loadRangeHistory =
   186:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
   187:     fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange;
   188: 
   189:   if (
   190:     requestedSource === "auto" ||
   191:     requestedSource === "snapshot"
   192:   ) {
   193:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
   194:       lotteryKey,
   195:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   196:     );
   197: 
   198:     const metadata =
   199:       metadataResult?.data || null;
   200: 
   201:     const snapshotReady =
   202:       metadataResult?.exists === true &&
   203:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus === "complete";
   204: 
   205:     if (snapshotReady) {
   206:       const draws = await loadFullHistory(
   207:         lotteryKey,
   208:         dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   209:       );
   210: 
   211:       if (Array.isArray(draws) && draws.length) {
   212:         const expectedTotal = Number(
   213:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0
   214:         );
   215: 
   216:         if (
   217:           expectedTotal > 0 &&
   218:           draws.length !== expectedTotal
   219:         ) {
   220:           throw new Error(
   221:             "Histórico TOP3 inconsistente: metadata.totalDraws=" +
   222:             `${expectedTotal}, carregados=${draws.length}.`
   223:           );
   224:         }
   225: 
   226:         return {
   227:           source: "snapshot",
   228:           draws,
   229:           metadata,
   230:           lookbackDays: null,
   231:           maxDraws: null,
   232:           startYmd:
   233:             metadata?.firstYmd || null,
   234:         };
   235:       }
   236: 
   237:       throw new Error(
   238:         "Metadata do histórico TOP3 está completo, mas nenhum draw foi carregado."
   239:       );
   240:     }
   241: 
   242:     if (requestedSource === "snapshot") {
   243:       throw new Error(
   244:         "Histórico TOP3 completo ainda não está disponível."
   245:       );
   246:     }
   247:   }
   248: 
   249:   const lookbackDays = Math.max(
   250:     30,
   251:     Math.min(
   252:       1460,
   253:       Number(input.lookbackDays || 180)
   254:     )
   255:   );
   256: 
   257:   const maxDraws = Math.max(
   258:     100,
   259:     Math.min(
   260:       5000,
   261:       Number(input.maxDraws || 1200)
   262:     )
   263:   );
   264: 
   265:   const startYmd = subtractDaysYmd(
   266:     date,
   267:     lookbackDays
   268:   );
   269: 
   270:   const rawDraws = await loadRangeHistory({
   271:     lottery: lotteryKey,
   272:     startYmd,
   273:     endYmd: date,
   274:     pageSize: Number(input.pageSize || 250),
   275:     maxDraws,
   276:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
   277:       input.prizeConcurrency || 24
   278:     ),
   279:   });
   280: 
   281:   return {
   282:     source: "range_fallback",
   283:     draws: extractDraws(rawDraws),
   284:     metadata: null,
   285:     lookbackDays,
   286:     maxDraws,
   287:     startYmd,
   288:   };
   289: }
   290: 
   291: function extractDraws(result) {
   292:   if (Array.isArray(result)) {
   293:     return result;
   294:   }
   295: 
   296:   const candidates = [
   297:     result?.draws,
   298:     result?.items,
   299:     result?.results,
   300:     result?.data,
   301:   ];
   302: 
   303:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
   304:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
   305:       return candidate;
   306:     }
   307:   }
   308: 
   309:   return [];
   310: }
   311: 
   312: function normalizePublicMilhar(value) {
   313:   const digits = String(value || "")
   314:     .replace(/\D+/g, "");
   315: 
   316:   if (!digits) {
   317:     return "";
   318:   }
   319: 
   320:   return digits
   321:     .slice(-4)
   322:     .padStart(4, "0");
   323: }
   324: 
   325: function publicHourCode(value) {
   326:   return normalizeHour(value)
   327:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
   328:     .slice(0, 2)
   329:     .padStart(2, "0");
   330: }
   331: 
   332: function resolveNextTop3Slot({
   333:   lotteryKey,
   334:   ymd,
   335:   hour,
   336:   publicApi,
   337: }) {
   338:   if (
   339:     !publicApi ||
   340:     typeof publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery !== "function"
   341:   ) {
   342:     throw new Error(
   343:       "API pública TOP3 sem getNextSlotForLottery."
   344:     );
   345:   }
   346: 
   347:   const resolved =
   348:     publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery({
   349:       lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey),
   350:       ymd: normalizeYmd(ymd),
   351:       hourBucket: normalizeHour(hour),
   352:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   353:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   354:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   355:     });
   356: 
   357:   const rawHour = String(
   358:     resolved?.hour ||
   359:     resolved?.hourBucket ||
   360:     ""
   361:   ).trim();
   362: 
   363:   const hourMatch =
   364:     rawHour.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::00|h)?$/i);
   365: 
   366:   const canonicalHour = hourMatch
   367:     ? `${String(Number(hourMatch[1])).padStart(2, "0")}:00`
   368:     : "";
   369: 
   370:   return {
   371:     ...resolved,
   372:     ymd: normalizeYmd(resolved?.ymd),
   373:     hour: canonicalHour,
   374:     hourBucket: canonicalHour,
   375:   };
   376: }
   377: 
   378: function scheduleForPublicProjection(
   379:   lotteryKey,
   380:   date
   381: ) {
   382:   if (normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey) === "FEDERAL") {
   383:     return [...FEDERAL_SCHEDULE];
   384:   }
   385: 
   386:   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
   387:   const dow = parsed.getUTCDay();
   388: 
   389:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) {
   390:     return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT];
   391:   }
   392: 
   393:   return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL];
   394: }
   395: 
   396: function buildPublicMilharesCols(
   397:   engineOutput,
   398:   expectedCols = 4,
   399:   perCol = 5
   400: ) {
   401:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.dezenas)
   402:     ? engineOutput.dezenas
   403:     : [];
   404: 
   405:   const slots = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.slots)
   406:     ? engineOutput.slots
   407:     : [];
   408: 
   409:   const cols = [];
   410: 
   411:   for (const dezena of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
   412:     const items = slots
   413:       .filter(
   414:         (slot) =>
   415:           String(slot?.dezena || "") ===
   416:           String(dezena || "")
   417:       )
   418:       .map(
   419:         (slot) =>
   420:           normalizePublicMilhar(slot?.milhar)
   421:       )
   422:       .filter(
   423:         (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
   424:       )
   425:       .slice(0, perCol);
   426: 
   427:     while (items.length < perCol) {
   428:       items.push("");
   429:     }
   430: 
   431:     cols.push({
   432:       dezena: String(dezena || ""),
   433:       items,
   434:     });
   435:   }
   436: 
   437:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
   438:     cols.push({
   439:       dezena: "",
   440:       items: Array(perCol).fill(""),
   441:     });
   442:   }
   443: 
   444:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
   445: }
   446: 
   447: function buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
   448:   computedTop,
   449:   history,
   450:   lotteryKey,
   451:   date,
   452:   closeHour,
   453:   publicApi,
   454: }) {
   455:   const schedule = scheduleForPublicProjection(
   456:     lotteryKey,
   457:     date
   458:   );
   459: 
   460:   return (Array.isArray(computedTop)
   461:     ? computedTop
   462:     : []
   463:   )
   464:     .slice(0, 3)
   465:     .map((item, index) => {
   466:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
   467: 
   468:       if (
   469:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
   470:         grupo < 1 ||
   471:         grupo > 25
   472:       ) {
   473:         return null;
   474:       }
   475: 
   476:       const probability = Number(
   477:         item?.scoreProb ??
   478:         item?.probability ??
   479:         item?.confidence ??
   480:         0
   481:       );
   482: 
   483:       const prob =
   484:         probability > 1
   485:           ? probability / 100
   486:           : probability;
   487: 
   488:       const engineOutput =
   489:         publicApi.build20MilharesForGrupo({
   490:           rangeDraws: history,
   491:           analysisHourBucket: closeHour,
   492:           schedule,
   493:           grupo2: grupo,
   494:           targetYmd: date,
   495:         });
   496: 
   497:       const milharesCols =
   498:         buildPublicMilharesCols(
   499:           engineOutput,
   500:           4,
   501:           5
   502:         );
   503: 
   504:       const milhares20 = milharesCols
   505:         .flatMap((column) => column.items)
   506:         .filter(
   507:           (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
   508:         )
   509:         .slice(0, 20);
   510: 
   511:       return {
   512:         rank: index + 1,
   513:         grupo,
   514:         animal:
   515:           item?.animal ||
   516:           ANIMALS[
   517:             String(grupo).padStart(2, "0")
   518:           ] ||
   519:           "",
   520:         prob:
   521:           Number.isFinite(prob)
   522:             ? prob
   523:             : 0,
   524:         probPct:
   525:           Number.isFinite(prob)
   526:             ? Number((prob * 100).toFixed(4))
   527:             : 0,
   528:         milhares20,
   529:         milharesCols,
   530:         meta: item?.meta || null,
   531:       };
   532:     })
   533:     .filter(Boolean);
   534: }
   535: 
   536: async function saveTop3PublicProjection({
   537:   lotteryKey,
   538:   date,
   539:   closeHour,
   540:   snapshot,
   541:   engineVersion = "V3_STATISTICAL",
   542:   source = "backend-top3",
   543: }) {
   544:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
   545:   const hour = normalizeHour(closeHour);
   546:   const hourCode = publicHourCode(hour);
   547: 
   548:   const id =
   549:     `${lottery}__${date}__${hourCode}`;
   550: 
   551:   const normalizedSnapshot =
   552:     Array.isArray(snapshot)
   553:       ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
   554:       : [];
   555: 
   556:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
   557:     throw new Error(
   558:       "Snapshot público TOP3 vazio."
   559:     );
   560:   }
   561: 
   562:   const picks = normalizedSnapshot
   563:     .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
   564:     .filter(
   565:       (grupo) =>
   566:         Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
   567:         grupo >= 1 &&
   568:         grupo <= 25
   569:     )
   570:     .slice(0, 3);
   571: 
   572:   if (!picks.length) {
   573:     throw new Error(
   574:       "Picks públicos TOP3 inválidos."
   575:     );
   576:   }
   577: 
   578:   const database = getDb();
   579:   const ref = database
   580:     .collection("top3_predictions")
   581:     .doc(id);
   582: 
   583:   const now = Date.now();
   584: 
   585:   const payload = {
   586:     id,
   587:     lotteryKey: lottery,
   588:     targetYmd: date,
   589:     targetHour:
   590:       `${hourCode}h`,
   591:     targetKey:
   592:       `${date}_${hourCode}h`,
   593:     picks,
   594:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
   595:     engineVersion,
   596:     status: "predicted",
   597:     resultGrupo: null,
   598:     resultMilhar: "",
   599:     resultAnimal: "",
   600:     hitType: "",
   601:     hitScore: 0,
   602:     hitPosition: -1,
   603:     matchedValue: "",
   604:     createdAt: now,
   605:     updatedAt: now,
   606:     createdBy: source,
   607:     source,
   608:   };
   609: 
   610:   return database.runTransaction(
   611:     async (transaction) => {
   612:       const current =
   613:         await transaction.get(ref);
   614: 
   615:       if (!current.exists) {
   616:         transaction.set(ref, payload);
   617: 
   618:         return {
   619:           ok: true,
   620:           created: true,
   621:           updated: false,
   622:           existing: false,
   623:           protected: false,
   624:           id,
   625:         };
   626:       }
   627: 
   628:       const currentData =
   629:         current.data() || {};
   630: 
   631:       const currentStatus = String(
   632:         currentData.status || ""
   633:       )
   634:         .trim()
   635:         .toLowerCase();
   636: 
   637:       if (currentStatus === "validated") {
   638:         return {
   639:           ok: true,
   640:           created: false,
   641:           updated: false,
   642:           existing: true,
   643:           protected: true,
   644:           reason: "ALREADY_VALIDATED",
   645:           id,
   646:         };
   647:       }
   648: 
   649:       const updatedPayload = {
   650:         ...payload,
   651:         createdAt:
   652:           currentData.createdAt ||
   653:           payload.createdAt,
   654:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
   655:         resultGrupo:
   656:           currentData.resultGrupo ?? null,
   657:         resultMilhar:
   658:           currentData.resultMilhar || "",
   659:         resultAnimal:
   660:           currentData.resultAnimal || "",
   661:         hitType:
   662:           currentData.hitType || "",
   663:         hitScore:
   664:           Number(currentData.hitScore || 0),
   665:         hitPosition:
   666:           Number.isFinite(
   667:             Number(currentData.hitPosition)
   668:           )
   669:             ? Number(currentData.hitPosition)
   670:             : -1,
   671:         matchedValue:
   672:           currentData.matchedValue || "",
   673:       };
   674: 
   675:       transaction.set(
   676:         ref,
   677:         updatedPayload,
   678:         { merge: true }
   679:       );
   680: 
   681:       return {
   682:         ok: true,
   683:         created: false,
   684:         updated: true,
   685:         existing: true,
   686:         protected: false,
   687:         id,
   688:       };
   689:     }
   690:   );
   691: }
   692: 
   693: function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
   694:   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
   695:     .slice(0, 3)
   696:     .map((item, index) => {
   697:       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);
   698:       const grupo = String(grupoNumber).padStart(2, "0");
   699: 
   700:       const probability = Number(
   701:         item?.scoreProb ??
   702:         item?.probability ??
   703:         item?.confidence ??
   704:         0
   705:       );
   706: 
   707:       const percentage =
   708:         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
   709:           ? probability * 100
   710:           : probability;
   711: 
   712:       return {
   713:         type: "grupo",
   714:         grupo,
   715:         animal:
   716:           item?.animal ||
   717:           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
   718:           null,
   719:         score: Number(
   720:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
   721:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
   722:             : 0
   723:         ),
   724:         confidence: Number(
   725:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
   726:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
   727:             : 0
   728:         ),
   729:         reasons: Array.isArray(item?.reasons)
   730:           ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean)
   731:           : [],
   732:         signals: {
   733:           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
   734:           rankPosition: index + 1,
   735:           scoreProb: Number(item?.scoreProb || 0),
   736:           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
   737:           meta: item?.meta || null,
   738:         },
   739:       };
   740:     })
   741:     .filter((item) => /^\d{2}$/.test(item.grupo));
   742: }
   743: 
   744: async function createTop3PredictionRun(
   745:   input = {},
   746:   dependencies = {}
   747: ) {
   748:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   749:     input.lotteryKey
   750:   );
   751: 
   752:   const date = normalizeYmd(input.date);
   753:   const closeHour = normalizeHour(input.closeHour);
   754: 
   755:   const computeTop3 =
   756:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
   757:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
   758: 
   759:   const persistRun =
   760:     dependencies.persistRun ||
   761:     createPredictionRun;
   762: 
   763:   const publicApi =
   764:     dependencies.publicApi ||
   765:     loadTop3PublicApi();
   766: 
   767:   const historyLoad = await loadPredictionHistory({
   768:     lotteryKey,
   769:     date,
   770:     input,
   771:     dependencies,
   772:   });
   773: 
   774:   const allDraws = extractDraws(
   775:     historyLoad.draws
   776:   );
   777: 
   778:   const {
   779:     source: historySource,
   780:     metadata: historyMetadata,
   781:     lookbackDays,
   782:     maxDraws,
   783:     startYmd,
   784:   } = historyLoad;
   785: 
   786:   if (!allDraws.length) {
   787:     throw new Error(
   788:       `Nenhum resultado encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
   789:     );
   790:   }
   791: 
   792:   const targetKey = dateHourKey(date, closeHour);
   793: 
   794:   const history = allDraws
   795:     .filter((draw) => {
   796:       const ymd = publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
   797:       const hour = publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
   798: 
   799:       if (!ymd || !hour) {
   800:         return false;
   801:       }
   802: 
   803:       return dateHourKey(ymd, hour) < targetKey;
   804:     })
   805:     .sort((a, b) => {
   806:       const aKey = dateHourKey(
   807:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(a),
   808:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(a)
   809:       );
   810: 
   811:       const bKey = dateHourKey(
   812:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(b),
   813:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(b)
   814:       );
   815: 
   816:       return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
   817:     });
   818: 
   819:   if (!history.length) {
   820:     throw new Error(
   821:       "Não existe histórico anterior ao horário solicitado."
   822:     );
   823:   }
   824: 
   825:   const drawLast = history[history.length - 1];
   826: 
   827:   const drawsToday = history.filter(
   828:     (draw) => publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw) === date
   829:   );
   830: 
   831:   const computed = computeTop3({
   832:     lotteryKey,
   833:     drawsRange: history,
   834:     drawLast,
   835:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   836:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   837:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   838:     topN: 3,
   839:     targetYmdOverride: date,
   840:     targetHourOverride: closeHour,
   841:   });
   842: 
   843:   const predictions = mapTop3ToPredictions(
   844:     computed?.top
   845:   );
   846: 
   847:   const publicSnapshot =
   848:     buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
   849:       computedTop: computed?.top,
   850:       history,
   851:       lotteryKey,
   852:       date,
   853:       closeHour,
   854:       publicApi,
   855:     });
   856: 
   857:   if (!predictions.length) {
   858:     throw new Error(
   859:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu previsões válidas."
   860:     );
   861:   }
   862: 
   863:   if (
   864:     publicSnapshot.length !== 3 ||
   865:     publicSnapshot.some(
   866:       (item) =>
   867:         !Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ||
   868:         item.milhares20.length !== 20
   869:     )
   870:   ) {
   871:     throw new Error(
   872:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu 20 milhares válidas para cada grupo."
   873:     );
   874:   }
   875: 
   876:   const metadata = {
   877:     ...(input.metadata || {}),
   878:     engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
   879:     historyDraws: history.length,
   880:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
   881:     historySource,
   882:     historyBootstrapStatus:
   883:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
   884:     historyTotalStored:
   885:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
   886:     lookbackDays,
   887:     maxDraws,
   888:     startYmd,
   889:     lastDrawYmd:
   890:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || null,
   891:     lastDrawHour:
   892:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(drawLast) || null,
   893:     targetYmd: date,
   894:     targetHour: closeHour,
   895:     engineMeta: computed?.meta || null,
   896:   };
   897: 
   898:   const engine = {
   899:     name: "top3_statistical_v3",
   900:     historyDraws: history.length,
   901:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
   902:     historySource,
   903:     historyBootstrapStatus:
   904:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
   905:     historyTotalStored:
   906:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
   907:     lookbackDays,
   908:     maxDraws,
   909:     startYmd,
   910:     targetYmd: date,
   911:     targetHour: closeHour,
   912:     meta: computed?.meta || null,
   913:   };
   914: 
   915:   if (input.dryRun === true) {
   916:     return {
   917:       run: null,
   918:       predictions,
   919:       publicSnapshot,
   920:       engine,
   921:       dryRun: true,
   922:     };
   923:   }
   924: 
   925:   const source =
   926:     input.source || "backend-top3";
   927: 
   928:   const result = await persistRun({
   929:     lotteryKey,
   930:     date,
   931:     closeHour,
   932:     source,
   933:     algorithm: "top3_statistical_v3",
   934:     metadata,
   935:     predictions,
   936:   });
   937: 
   938:   const publicProjection =
   939:     await saveTop3PublicProjection({
   940:       lotteryKey,
   941:       date,
   942:       closeHour,
   943:       snapshot: publicSnapshot,
   944:       engineVersion: "V3_STATISTICAL",
   945:       source,
   946:     });
   947: 
   948:   return {
   949:     ...result,
   950:     publicSnapshot,
   951:     publicProjection,
   952:     engine,
   953:     dryRun: false,
   954:   };
   955: }
   956: 
   957: module.exports = {
   958:   createTop3PredictionRun,
   959:   mapTop3ToPredictions,
   960:   normalizeHour,
   961:   normalizeHistorySource,
   962:   loadPredictionHistory,
   963:   buildPublicMilharesCols,
   964:   buildTop3PublicSnapshot,
   965:   saveTop3PublicProjection,
   966:   resolveNextTop3Slot,

ARQUIVO: .\backend\workers\bootstrapTop3History.js

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const {
     4:   fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes,
     5: } = require("../engine/drawRepository");
     6: 
     7: const {
     8:   normalizeLotteryKey,
     9:   normalizeDraw,
    10:   deduplicateDraws,
    11:   writeHistoryMonth,
    12:   writeMetadata,
    13:   SCHEMA_VERSION,
    14: } = require("../engine/top3HistoryRepository");
    15: 
    16: const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 500;
    17: const DEFAULT_PRIZE_CONCURRENCY = 24;
    18: 
    19: /**
    20:  * Margem abaixo do limite de 1 MiB do Firestore.
    21:  * O bootstrap é interrompido antes de tentar gravar
    22:  * um documento mensal excessivamente grande.
    23:  */
    24: const MAX_MONTH_DOCUMENT_BYTES = 850 * 1024;
    25: 
    26: function safeArray(value) {
    27:   return Array.isArray(value)
    28:     ? value
    29:     : [];
    30: }
    31: 
    32: function estimateJsonBytes(value) {
    33:   return Buffer.byteLength(
    34:     JSON.stringify(value),
    35:     "utf8"
    36:   );
    37: }
    38: 
    39: function groupDrawsByMonth(draws = []) {
    40:   const grouped = new Map();
    41: 
    42:   for (const rawDraw of safeArray(draws)) {
    43:     const draw = normalizeDraw(rawDraw);
    44: 
    45:     if (!draw) {
    46:       continue;
    47:     }
    48: 
    49:     const yearMonth = draw.ymd.slice(0, 7);
    50: 
    51:     if (!grouped.has(yearMonth)) {
    52:       grouped.set(yearMonth, []);
    53:     }
    54: 
    55:     grouped.get(yearMonth).push(draw);
    56:   }
    57: 
    58:   const normalized = new Map();
    59: 
    60:   for (
    61:     const [yearMonth, monthDraws]
    62:     of grouped.entries()
    63:   ) {
    64:     normalized.set(
    65:       yearMonth,
    66:       deduplicateDraws(monthDraws)
    67:     );
    68:   }
    69: 
    70:   return normalized;
    71: }
    72: 
    73: function buildBootstrapPlan({
    74:   lotteryKey,
    75:   draws,
    76: } = {}) {
    77:   const key = normalizeLotteryKey(
    78:     lotteryKey
    79:   );
    80: 
    81:   const normalizedDraws = deduplicateDraws(
    82:     safeArray(draws)
    83:   ).filter(
    84:     (draw) =>
    85:       draw.lotteryKey === key
    86:   );
    87: 
    88:   const grouped = groupDrawsByMonth(
    89:     normalizedDraws
    90:   );
    91: 
    92:   const months = Array.from(
    93:     grouped.keys()
    94:   ).sort();
    95: 
    96:   const monthPlans = months.map(
    97:     (yearMonth) => {
    98:       const monthDraws =
    99:         grouped.get(yearMonth) || [];
   100: 
   101:       const first =
   102:         monthDraws[0] || null;
   103: 
   104:       const last =
   105:         monthDraws[
   106:           monthDraws.length - 1
   107:         ] || null;
   108: 
   109:       const estimatedBytes =
   110:         estimateJsonBytes({
   111:           schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,
   112:           lotteryKey: key,
   113:           yearMonth,
   114:           drawCount: monthDraws.length,
   115:           firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
   116:           lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
   117:           firstDrawId:
   118:             first?.drawId || null,
   119:           lastDrawId:
   120:             last?.drawId || null,
   121:           draws: monthDraws,
   122:         });
   123: 
   124:       return {
   125:         yearMonth,
   126:         draws: monthDraws,
   127:         drawCount: monthDraws.length,
   128:         firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
   129:         lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
   130:         firstDrawId:
   131:           first?.drawId || null,
   132:         lastDrawId:
   133:           last?.drawId || null,
   134:         estimatedBytes,
   135:         withinSizeLimit:
   136:           estimatedBytes <=
   137:           MAX_MONTH_DOCUMENT_BYTES,
   138:       };
   139:     }
   140:   );
   141: 
   142:   const oversizedMonths =
   143:     monthPlans.filter(
   144:       (month) =>
   145:         !month.withinSizeLimit
   146:     );
   147: 
   148:   const first =
   149:     normalizedDraws[0] || null;
   150: 
   151:   const last =
   152:     normalizedDraws[
   153:       normalizedDraws.length - 1
   154:     ] || null;
   155: 
   156:   return {
   157:     lotteryKey: key,
   158:     draws: normalizedDraws,
   159:     totalDraws:
   160:       normalizedDraws.length,
   161:     months,
   162:     monthPlans,
   163:     oversizedMonths,
   164:     firstYmd:
   165:       first?.ymd || null,
   166:     lastYmd:
   167:       last?.ymd || null,
   168:     firstDrawId:
   169:       first?.drawId || null,
   170:     lastDrawId:
   171:       last?.drawId || null,
   172:   };
   173: }
   174: 
   175: async function bootstrapTop3History(
   176:   options = {},
   177:   dependencies = {}
   178: ) {
   179:   const lotteryKey =
   180:     normalizeLotteryKey(
   181:       options.lotteryKey ||
   182:       "PT_RIO"
   183:     );
   184: 
   185:   const dryRun =
   186:     options.dryRun !== false;
   187: 
   188:   const fetchDraws =
   189:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
   190:     fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes;
   191: 
   192:   const persistMonth =
   193:     dependencies.writeHistoryMonth ||
   194:     writeHistoryMonth;
   195: 
   196:   const persistMetadata =
   197:     dependencies.writeMetadata ||
   198:     writeMetadata;
   199: 
   200:   const startedAt = Date.now();
   201: 
   202:   console.log(
   203:     `[TOP3-HISTORY] Iniciando bootstrap ${lotteryKey} | dryRun=${dryRun}`
   204:   );
   205: 
   206:   const rawDraws = await fetchDraws({
   207:     lottery: lotteryKey,
   208:     pageSize: Number(
   209:       options.pageSize ||
   210:       DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE
   211:     ),
   212:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
   213:       options.prizeConcurrency ||
   214:       DEFAULT_PRIZE_CONCURRENCY
   215:     ),
   216:   });
   217: 
   218:   const plan = buildBootstrapPlan({
   219:     lotteryKey,
   220:     draws: rawDraws,
   221:   });
   222: 
   223:   if (!plan.totalDraws) {
   224:     throw new Error(
   225:       `Nenhum draw válido encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
   226:     );
   227:   }
   228: 
   229:   if (plan.oversizedMonths.length) {
   230:     const details =
   231:       plan.oversizedMonths
   232:         .map(
   233:           (month) =>
   234:             `${month.yearMonth}=${month.estimatedBytes} bytes`
   235:         )
   236:         .join(", ");
   237: 
   238:     throw new Error(
   239:       "Bootstrap bloqueado: documento mensal acima do limite seguro. " +
   240:       details
   241:     );
   242:   }
   243: 
   244:   console.log(
   245:     `[TOP3-HISTORY] Draws=${plan.totalDraws} | meses=${plan.months.length}`
   246:   );
   247: 
   248:   for (const month of plan.monthPlans) {
   249:     console.log(
   250:       `[TOP3-HISTORY] ${month.yearMonth} | draws=${month.drawCount} | bytes≈${month.estimatedBytes}`
   251:     );
   252:   }
   253: 
   254:   if (dryRun) {
   255:     return {
   256:       ok: true,
   257:       dryRun: true,
   258:       lotteryKey,
   259:       totalDraws:
   260:         plan.totalDraws,
   261:       months:
   262:         plan.months,
   263:       monthCount:
   264:         plan.months.length,
   265:       firstYmd:
   266:         plan.firstYmd,
   267:       lastYmd:
   268:         plan.lastYmd,
   269:       firstDrawId:
   270:         plan.firstDrawId,
   271:       lastDrawId:
   272:         plan.lastDrawId,
   273:       oversizedMonths: [],
   274:       tookMs:
   275:         Date.now() - startedAt,
   276:     };
   277:   }
   278: 
   279:   for (const month of plan.monthPlans) {
   280:     await persistMonth(
   281:       lotteryKey,
   282:       month.yearMonth,
   283:       month.draws,
   284:       dependencies
   285:     );
   286: 
   287:     console.log(
   288:       `[TOP3-HISTORY] Gravado ${month.yearMonth}: ${month.drawCount} draws`
   289:     );
   290:   }
   291: 
   292:   const completedAt =
   293:     new Date().toISOString();
   294: 
   295:   const metadata =
   296:     await persistMetadata(
   297:       lotteryKey,
   298:       {
   299:         bootstrapStatus:
   300:           "complete",
   301:         bootstrapCompletedAt:
   302:           completedAt,
   303:         totalDraws:
   304:           plan.totalDraws,
   305:         monthCount:
   306:           plan.months.length,
   307:         months:
   308:           plan.months,
   309:         firstYmd:
   310:           plan.firstYmd,
   311:         lastYmd:
   312:           plan.lastYmd,
   313:         firstDrawId:
   314:           plan.firstDrawId,
   315:         lastDrawId:
   316:           plan.lastDrawId,
   317:         lastProcessedDrawId:
   318:           plan.lastDrawId,
   319:         source:
   320:           "fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes",
   321:       },
   322:       dependencies
   323:     );
   324: 
   325:   return {
   326:     ok: true,
   327:     dryRun: false,
   328:     lotteryKey,
   329:     totalDraws:
   330:       plan.totalDraws,
   331:     months:
   332:       plan.months,
   333:     monthCount:
   334:       plan.months.length,
   335:     firstYmd:
   336:       plan.firstYmd,
   337:     lastYmd:
   338:       plan.lastYmd,
   339:     firstDrawId:
   340:       plan.firstDrawId,
   341:     lastDrawId:
   342:       plan.lastDrawId,
   343:     metadata,
   344:     tookMs:
   345:       Date.now() - startedAt,
   346:   };
   347: }
   348: 
   349: function parseCliArgs(argv = []) {
   350:   const lotteryKey =
   351:     String(argv[0] || "PT_RIO")
   352:       .trim()
   353:       .toUpperCase();
   354: 
   355:   const flags = new Set(
   356:     argv.slice(1).map(
   357:       (value) =>
   358:         String(value)
   359:           .trim()
   360:           .toLowerCase()
   361:     )
   362:   );
   363: 
   364:   const commit =
   365:     flags.has("--commit");
   366: 
   367:   return {
   368:     lotteryKey,
   369:     dryRun: !commit,
   370:   };
   371: }
   372: 
   373: async function main() {
   374:   const options = parseCliArgs(
   375:     process.argv.slice(2)
   376:   );
   377: 
   378:   console.log("");
   379:   console.log(
   380:     "===== TOP3 HISTORY BOOTSTRAP ====="
   381:   );
   382:   console.log(
   383:     "Loteria:",
   384:     options.lotteryKey
   385:   );
   386:   console.log(
   387:     "Modo....:",
   388:     options.dryRun
   389:       ? "DRY-RUN"
   390:       : "COMMIT"
   391:   );
   392:   console.log("");
   393: 
   394:   const result =
   395:     await bootstrapTop3History(
   396:       options
   397:     );
   398: 
   399:   console.log("");
   400:   console.log(
   401:     JSON.stringify(
   402:       result,
   403:       null,
   404:       2
   405:     )
   406:   );
   407: }
   408: 
   409: if (require.main === module) {
   410:   main().catch((error) => {
   411:     console.error(
   412:       "ERRO:",
   413:       error?.stack ||
   414:       error?.message ||
   415:       error
   416:     );
   417: 
   418:     process.exit(1);
   419:   });
   420: }

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: /**
     4:  * importKingApostas.js
     5:  * (topo limpo automaticamente — removido bloco corrompido com \n e \)
     6:  */
     7: const fs = require("fs");
     8: const path = require("path");
     9: const axios = require("axios");
    10: const {
    11:   normalizeFederalRequestedSlot,
    12:   normalizeFederalSourceSlot,
    13: } = require("./federalCalendar");
    14: 
    15: /**
    16:  * =========================
    17:  * ENV helpers
    18:  * =========================
    19:  */
    20: 
    21: /**
    22:  * Carrega variáveis do backend/.env.local (se existir)
    23:  * Observação: só define se a env ainda não estiver definida no processo.
    24:  */
    25: (function loadEnvLocal() {
    26:   try {
    27:     const envPath = path.join(__dirname, "..", ".env.local");
    28:     if (!fs.existsSync(envPath)) return;
    29: 
    30:     let raw = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
    31:     raw = raw.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ""); // remove BOM invisível
    32: 
    33:     raw.split(/\r?\n/).forEach((line) => {
    34:       let s = String(line || "").trim();
    35:       if (!s || s.startsWith("#")) return;
    36: 
    37:       // suporta "export KEY=VAL"
    38:       if (/^export\s+/i.test(s)) s = s.replace(/^export\s+/i, "").trim();
    39: 
    40:       const i = s.indexOf("=");
    41:       if (i <= 0) return;
    42: 
    43:       const k = s.slice(0, i).trim();
    44:       let v = s.slice(i + 1).trim();
    45: 
    46:       // suporta KEY="valor" / KEY='valor'
    47:       if (
    48:         (v.startsWith('"') && v.endsWith('"')) ||
    49:         (v.startsWith("'") && v.endsWith("'"))
    50:       ) {
    51:         v = v.slice(1, -1);
    52:       }
    53: 
    54:       if (k && v && !process.env[k]) process.env[k] = v;
    55:     });
    56:   } catch {
    57:     // silencioso por design
    58:   }
    59: })();
    60: 
    61: /**
    62:  * Resolve automaticamente credenciais se:
    63:  * - GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS estiver ausente, OU
    64:  * - estiver apontando para um arquivo inexistente.
    65:  *
    66:  * Mantém compatível com seu fluxo local (Windows) e com Cloud (ADC).
    67:  */
    68: (function ensureGoogleCredentials() {
    69:   try {
    70:     const current = String(process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS || "").trim();
    71: 
    72:     // se já está OK, não mexe
    73:     if (current) {
    74:       try {
    75:         if (fs.existsSync(current) && fs.lstatSync(current).isFile()) return;
    76:       } catch {
    77:         // segue para fallback
    78:       }
    79:     }
    80: 
    81:     // se existe mas está inválida, remove para permitir fallback (ADC)
    82:     if (current) {
    83:       try {
    84:         delete process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS;
    85:       } catch {
    86:         process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = "";
    87:       }
    88:     }
    89: 
    90:     const tryPickFromDir = (dir) => {
    91:       try {
    92:         if (!dir || !fs.existsSync(dir)) return null;
    93: 
    94:         const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    95:         const hits = entries
    96:           .filter((f) => /^palpitacojb-app-firebase-adminsdk-.*\.json$/i.test(f))
    97:           .map((f) => path.join(dir, f))
    98:           .filter((p) => {
    99:             try {
   100:               return fs.existsSync(p) && fs.lstatSync(p).isFile();
   101:             } catch {
   102:               return false;
   103:             }
   104:           });
   105: 
   106:         if (!hits.length) return null;
   107: 
   108:         hits.sort((a, b) => {
   109:           try {
   110:             return fs.statSync(b).mtimeMs - fs.statSync(a).mtimeMs;
   111:           } catch {
   112:             return 0;
   113:           }
   114:         });
   115: 
   116:         return hits[0] || null;
   117:       } catch {
   118:         return null;
   119:       }
   120:     };
   121: 
   122:     // 1) tenta no CWD
   123:     const cwdPick = tryPickFromDir(process.cwd());
   124:     if (cwdPick) {
   125:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = cwdPick;
   126:       return;
   127:     }
   128: 
   129:     // 2) tenta subir alguns níveis
   130:     const up1 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", ".."); // .../archive_backend
   131:     const up2 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", "..", ".."); // .../palpitaco (provável)
   132:     const pick1 = tryPickFromDir(up2) || tryPickFromDir(up1);
   133: 
   134:     if (pick1) {
   135:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = pick1;
   136:       return;
   137:     }
   138:   } catch {
   139:     // silencioso por design
   140:   }
   141: })();
   142: 
   143: /**
   144:  * =========================
   145:  * Firebase Admin (central)
   146:  * =========================
   147:  *
   148:  * Importante:
   149:  * este require vem DEPOIS do ensureGoogleCredentials/loadEnvLocal
   150:  */
   151: const { admin, db } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
   152: 
   153: const { getPtRioSlotsByDate } = require("./ptRioCalendar");
   154: 
   155: const {
   156:   syncImportedResultToTop3History,
   157: } = require("../engine/top3HistorySync");
   158: 
   159: const {
   160:   createTop3PredictionRun,
   161:   resolveNextTop3Slot,
   162: } = require("../engine/top3PredictionService");
   163: 
   164: const {
   165:   loadTop3PublicApi,
   166: } = require("../engine/scoreEngineUnified");
   167: 
   168: /**
   169:  * =========================
   170:  * Config / toggles
   171:  * =========================
   172:  */
   173: 
   174: /**
   175:  * Para desempenho/estabilidade:
   176:  * - CHECK_EXISTENCE=1 => faz reads extras para calcular "novos" (lento)
   177:  * - default => não faz reads extras (rápido)
   178:  */
   179: const CHECK_EXISTENCE = String(process.env.CHECK_EXISTENCE || "").trim() === "1";
   180: 
   181: /**
   182:  * Retry HTTP simples (evita falhas intermitentes/429)
   183:  */
   184: const HTTP_RETRIES = Number.isFinite(Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRIES))
   185:   ? Math.max(0, Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRIES))
   186:   : 3;
   187: 
   188: const HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS = Number.isFinite(Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS))
   189:   ? Math.max(50, Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS))
   190:   : 600;
   191: 
   192: /**
   193:  * ✅ fetch por lottery (robusto contra “cap” da API quando manda muitos lotteries[]).
   194:  * Default: ON
   195:  * - KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY=0 => volta ao modo antigo (1 request com todos lotteries[])
   196:  */
   197: const FETCH_PER_LOTTERY =
   198:   String(process.env.KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY || "").trim() === "0" ? false : true;
   199: 
   200: // ✅ Debug do fetch (log por lotteryId)
   201: // Ative com: set KING_FETCH_DEBUG=1
   202: const KING_FETCH_DEBUG = String(process.env.KING_FETCH_DEBUG || "").trim() === "1";
   203: 
   204: // ✅ Debug leve do override FEDERAL
   205: // Ative com: set KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG=1
   206: const KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG = String(process.env.KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG || "").trim() === "1";
   207: 
   208: /**
   209:  * ✅ Blindagem contra datas futuras (não busca API, não escreve no FS)
   210:  * - ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE=1 => libera (use com MUITO cuidado / apenas para testes)
   211:  */
   212: const ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE = String(process.env.ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE || "").trim() === "1";
   213: 
   214: /**
   215:  * =========================
   216:  * ✅ Regras de negócio: UF x lottery_key
   217:  * =========================
   218:  *
   219:  * IMPORTANTE:
   220:  * - lottery_key identifica a loteria/fonte (ex.: PT_RIO)
   221:  * - uf deve ser o estado (ex.: RJ), pois o frontend consulta por UF ("RJ")
   222:  *
   223:  * Se uf ficar "PT_RIO", o bounds por UF quebra.
   224:  */
   225: const RJ_STATE_CODE = "RJ";
   226: const RJ_LOTTERY_KEY = "PT_RIO";
   227: 
   228: const FEDERAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
   229: const FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "FEDERAL";
   230: 
   231: const LOOK_STATE_CODE = "GO";
   232: const LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY = "LOOK";
   233: 
   234: const NACIONAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
   235: const NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "NACIONAL";
   236: 
   237: function resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lotteryKey) {
   238:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   239:   if (!lk) return null;
   240:   if (lk === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) return RJ_STATE_CODE;
   241:   if (lk === FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return FEDERAL_STATE_CODE;
   242:   if (lk === LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY) return LOOK_STATE_CODE;
   243:   if (lk === NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return NACIONAL_STATE_CODE;
   244: 
   245:   // se for uma UF padrão de 2 letras (SP, MG, DF etc.), preserva
   246:   if (/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(lk)) return lk;
   247: 
   248:   // fallback: não inventa UF
   249:   return null;
   250: }
   251: 
   252: /**
   253:  * =========================
   254:  * Date helpers (BRT via TZ)
   255:  * =========================
   256:  */
   257: 
   258: function pad2(n) {
   259:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   260: }
   261: 
   262: function todayYMDLocal() {
   263:   try {
   264:     const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
   265:       timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
   266:       year: "numeric",
   267:       month: "2-digit",
   268:       day: "2-digit",
   269:     });
   270:     return fmt.format(new Date()); // YYYY-MM-DD
   271:   } catch {
   272:     const d = new Date();
   273:     return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   274:   }
   275: }
   276: 
   277: function isFutureISODate(ymd) {
   278:   // Compare lexicográfico funciona para YYYY-MM-DD
   279:   const t = todayYMDLocal();
   280:   return String(ymd || "").trim() > t;
   281: }

   289: /** Mapeamento oficial Grupo -> Animal */
   290: const GRUPO_TO_ANIMAL = {
   291:   1: "Avestruz",
   292:   2: "Águia",
   293:   3: "Burro",
   294:   4: "Borboleta",
   295:   5: "Cachorro",
   296:   6: "Cabra",
   297:   7: "Carneiro",
   298:   8: "Camelo",
   299:   9: "Cobra",
   300:   10: "Coelho",

   303:   13: "Galo",
   304:   14: "Gato",
   305:   15: "Jacaré",
   306:   16: "Leão",
   307:   17: "Macaco",

   309:   19: "Pavão",
   310:   20: "Peru",
   311:   21: "Touro",

   313:   23: "Urso",
   314:   24: "Veado",
   315:   25: "Vaca",

   318: function pad4(s) {
   319:   return String(s ?? "").trim().padStart(4, "0");
   320: }
   321: 
   322: /** Regra do Jogo do Bicho: dezena 00 => grupo 25; senão ceil(dezena/4) */
   323: function dezenaToGrupo(dezena2) {
   324:   const n = Number(dezena2);
   325:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
   326:   if (n === 0) return 25;
   327:   return Math.ceil(n / 4);
   328: }
   329: 
   330: function normalizePrize(raw) {
   331:   const milhar = pad4(raw);
   332:   const centena = milhar.slice(-3);
   333:   const dezena = milhar.slice(-2);
   334:   const grupo = dezenaToGrupo(dezena);
   335:   const animal = grupo ? GRUPO_TO_ANIMAL[grupo] : null;
   336:   return { raw: String(raw), milhar, centena, dezena, grupo, animal };
   337: }
   338: 
   339: function isISODate(s) {
   340:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   341: }
   342: 
   343: function isHHMM(s) {
   344:   return /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   345: }
   346: 
   347: /**
   348:  * Normaliza horário para HH:MM (raw)
   349:  * Aceita: "10", "10h", "10:9", "10:09"
   350:  */
   351: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
   352:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   353:   if (!s) return "";
   354: 
   355:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
   356: 
   357:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   358:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   359: 
   360:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   361:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   362: 
   363:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
   364:   if (m3) {
   365:     const hh = String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0");
   366:     const mm = String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0");
   367:     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
   368:   }
   369: 
   370:   return "";
   371: }
   372: 
   373: /**
   374:  * ✅ Normaliza close_hour para SLOT (campo de negócio)
   375:  * - Para PT_RIO: força minutos = 00 (09:09 -> 09:00)
   376:  * - Para outros: preserva raw
   377:  *
   378:  * Correção crítica:
   379:  * - A API do PT_RIO costuma devolver HH:09 como "marcação" e NÃO um minuto real.
   380:  * - Portanto, para PT_RIO, se raw terminar em ":09", tratamos como "sem minuto real"
   381:  *   e NÃO persistimos close_hour_raw (vira "").
   382:  *
   383:  * Retorna:
   384:  * - raw: HH:MM (normalizado) OU "" quando for "sintético" (PT_RIO HH:09)
   385:  * - slot: HH:MM (normalizado para o que o sistema deve usar em consultas/ID)
   386:  */
   387: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(value, lotteryKey) {
   388:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   389:   const raw0 = normalizeHHMM(value);
   390:   if (!raw0 || !isHHMM(raw0)) return { raw: "", slot: "" };
   391: 
   392:   // Federal: 11h e o novo slot oficial de domingo.
   393:   // A marcacao antecipada de 19h continua pertencendo ao slot 20h.
   394:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   395:     return {
   396:       raw: raw0,
   397:       slot: normalizeFederalRequestedSlot(raw0),
   398:     };
   399:   }
   400: 
   401:   // LOOK:
   402:   // a fonte pode publicar minutos variáveis, por exemplo 07:09, 09:09 e 11:12.
   403:   // Para o sistema, todos pertencem ao slot operacional da hora cheia.
   404:   // Preservamos o horário bruto em close_hour_raw e gravamos HH:00 em close_hour.
   405:   if (lk === "LOOK") {
   406:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
   407:     return { raw: raw0, slot: `${hh}:00` };
   408:   }
   409: 
   410:   if (lk === "PT_RIO") {
   411:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
   412:     const mm = raw0.slice(3, 5);
   413: 
   414:     // ✅ se vier HH:09, é marcação (não minuto real) -> não grava close_hour_raw
   415:     const raw = mm === "09" ? "" : raw0;
   416:     return { raw, slot: `${hh}:00` };
   417:   }
   418: 
   419:   return { raw: raw0, slot: raw0 };
   420: }
   421: 
   422: 
   423: /**
   424:  * NACIONAL:
   425:  * usa lottery_name para obter o horário oficial do sorteio.
   426:  */
   427: function normalizeDrawCloseHour(draw, lotteryKey) {
   428:   const base = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(
   429:     draw?.close_hour || "",
   430:     lotteryKey
   431:   );
   432: 
   433:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   434: 
   435:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   436:     return {
   437:       raw: base.raw,
   438:       slot: normalizeFederalSourceSlot({
   439:         date: draw?.date || draw?.ymd || "",
   440:         rawSlot: base.raw || base.slot,
   441:       }),
   442:     };
   443:   }
   444: 
   445:   if (lk !== "NACIONAL") {
   446:     return base;
   447:   }
   448: 
   449:   const lotteryName = String(
   450:     draw?.lottery_name ||
   451:     draw?.name ||
   452:     ""
   453:   );
   454: 
   455:   const m = lotteryName.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*HS/i);
   456: 
   457:   if (!m) {
   458:     return base;
   459:   }
   460: 
   461:   return {
   462:     raw: base.raw,
   463:     slot: `${String(m[1]).padStart(2,"0")}:00`
   464:   };
   465: }
   466: 
   467: 
   468: /**
   469:  * ✅ Normaliza o parâmetro closeHour (do CLI/scheduler) e evita log enganoso.
   470:  * - Para FEDERAL: qualquer coisa vira slot=20:00, mas mantemos "requested" para log.
   471:  */
   472: function normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lotteryKey) {
   473:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   474:   const requested = closeHour ? normalizeHHMM(closeHour) : null;
   475:   const requestedIsValid = requested ? isHHMM(requested) : false;
   476: 
   477:   if (!requested) {
   478:     return { requested: null, slot: null, note: null };
   479:   }
   480:   if (!requestedIsValid) {
   481:     return { requested, slot: null, note: "invalid" };
   482:   }
   483: 
   484:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   485:     const slot =
   486:       normalizeFederalRequestedSlot(requested);
   487: 
   488:     const note =
   489:       requested !== slot
   490:         ? `FEDERAL_slot_normalized(${requested}->${slot})`
   491:         : null;
   492: 
   493:     return { requested, slot, note };
   494:   }
   495: 
   496:   // outros: normalização normal por lotteryKey (PT_RIO min->00 etc.)
   497:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(requested, lk).slot;
   498:   return { requested, slot: slot || null, note: null };
   499: }
   500: 
   501: /**
   502:  * Sanitiza string para docId do Firestore.
   503:  */
   504: function safeIdPart(input) {
   505:   const s = String(input ?? "").trim();
   506:   if (!s) return "NA";
   507:   return s
   508:     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
   509:     .replace(/[\/\\?#\[\]]/g, "_")
   510:     .replace(/:+/g, "-")
   511:     .slice(0, 80);
   512: }
   513: 
   514: function pickLotteryId(draw) {
   515:   const candidates = [
   516:     draw?.lottery_id,
   517:     draw?.lotteryId,
   518:     draw?.lottery_uuid,
   519:     draw?.lotteryUuid,
   520:     draw?.lottery,
   521:     draw?.lottery_key,
   522:   ];
   523:   for (const c of candidates) {
   524:     const s = String(c ?? "").trim();
   525:     if (s) return s;
   526:   }
   527:   return null;
   528: }
