   574: (function applyLotteryOverridesFromEnv() {
   575:   try {
   576:     const rawRio = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_PT_RIO || "").trim();
   577:     if (rawRio) {
   578:       const arr = rawRio
   579:         .split(",")
   580:         .map((s) => String(s || "").trim())
   581:         .filter(Boolean);
   582:       if (arr.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.PT_RIO = arr;
   583:     }
   584: 
   585:     const rawFed = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_FEDERAL || "").trim();
   586:     if (rawFed) {
   587:       const arrF = rawFed
   588:         .split(",")
   589:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   590:         .filter(Boolean);
   591:       if (arrF.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.FEDERAL = arrF;
   592:     }
   593: 
   594:     const rawLook = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_LOOK || "").trim();
   595:     if (rawLook) {
   596:       const arrLook = rawLook
   597:         .split(",")
   598:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   599:         .filter(Boolean);
   600:       if (arrLook.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.LOOK = arrLook;
   601:     }
   602: 
   603:     const rawNacional = String(
   604:       process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_NACIONAL || ""
   605:     ).trim();
   606:     if (rawNacional) {
   607:       const arrNacional = rawNacional
   608:         .split(",")
   609:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   610:         .filter(Boolean);
   611:       if (arrNacional.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.NACIONAL = arrNacional;
   612:     }
   613: 
   614:     // debug leve (único, no lugar certo)
   615:     if (KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG) {
   616:       const counts = Object.fromEntries(
   617:         Object.entries(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY).map(([key, value]) => [
   618:           key,
   619:           Array.isArray(value) ? value.length : 0,
   620:         ])
   621:       );
   622: 
   623:       console.log(
   624:         `[OVERRIDE] PT_RIO lotteries=${counts.PT_RIO} | ` +
   625:           `FEDERAL lotteries=${counts.FEDERAL} | ` +
   626:           `LOOK lotteries=${counts.LOOK} | ` +
   627:           `NACIONAL lotteries=${counts.NACIONAL}`
   628:       );
   629:     }
   630:   } catch {
   631:     // silencioso
   632:   }
   633: })();
   634: 
   635: function buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey, lotteryId = null }) {
   636:   const base = "https://app_services.apionline.cloud/api/results";
   637:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   638:   const lotteries = LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk];
   639: 
   640:   if (!lotteries?.length) {
   641:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY, lk)) {
   642:       throw new Error(
   643:         `${lk} sem lotteryIds. Defina KING_LOTTERIES_${lk}="uuid1,uuid2,...".`
   644:       );
   645:     }
   646: 
   647:     throw new Error(`lotteryKey inválida: ${lk}`);
   648:   }
   649: 
   650:   const params = new URLSearchParams();
   651:   params.append("dates[]", date);
   652: 
   653:   // ✅ se lotteryId for fornecido -> 1 request por lottery
   654:   if (lotteryId) {
   655:     params.append("lotteries[]", lotteryId);
   656:     return `${base}?${params.toString()}`;
   657:   }
   658: 
   659:   // modo antigo (todos lotteries no mesmo request)
   660:   for (const id of lotteries) params.append("lotteries[]", id);
   661:   return `${base}?${params.toString()}`;
   662: }
   663: 
   664: function sleep(ms) {
   665:   return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
   666: }
   667: 
   668: function shouldRetryAxiosError(err) {
   669:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
   670:   const status = Number(err?.response?.status);
   671: 
   672:   if (
   673:     code.includes("etimedout") ||
   674:     code.includes("econnreset") ||
   675:     code.includes("enotfound")
   676:   ) {
   677:     return true;
   678:   }
   679: 
   680:   // 429 / 5xx
   681:   if (
   682:     Number.isFinite(status) &&
   683:     (status === 429 || (status >= 500 && status <= 599))
   684:   ) {
   685:     return true;
   686:   }
   687: 
   688:   return false;
   689: }
   690: 
   691: async function axiosGetJson(url) {
   692:   let lastErr = null;
   693: 
   694:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= HTTP_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   695:     try {
   696:       const { data } = await axios.get(url, {
   697:         headers: {
   698:           Accept: "application/json, text/plain, */*",
   699:           Origin: "https://app.kingapostas.com",
   700:           Referer: "https://app.kingapostas.com/",
   701:         },
   702:         timeout: 30000,
   703:         validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 300,
   704:       });
   705: 
   706:       if (!data?.success || !Array.isArray(data?.data)) {
   707:         throw new Error("Resposta inesperada da API (success/data).");
   708:       }
   709: 
   710:       return data;
   711:     } catch (e) {
   712:       lastErr = e;
   713: 
   714:       const canRetry = attempt < HTTP_RETRIES && shouldRetryAxiosError(e);
   715:       if (!canRetry) break;
   716: 
   717:       const backoff = HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, attempt);
   718:       console.warn(
   719:         `[HTTP] tentativa ${attempt + 1}/${HTTP_RETRIES + 1} falhou (${
   720:           e?.response?.status || e?.code || "err"
   721:         }). retry em ${backoff}ms...`
   722:       );
   723:       await sleep(backoff);
   724:     }
   725:   }
   726: 
   727:   throw lastErr || new Error("Falha ao buscar API.");
   728: }
   729: 
   730: /* =========================
   731:    DETAILS (HTML) fallback — PT_RIO 18h
   732: ========================= */
   733: function buildDetailsUrl({ date, lotteryKey }) {
   734:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   735: 
   736:   // ✅ bate com o print: lottery=PT+RIO
   737:   const lot = lk === "PT_RIO" ? "PT RIO" : lk.replace(/_/g, " ");
   738: 
   739:   const q =
   740:     "lottery=" +
   741:     encodeURIComponent(lot) +
   742:     "&date=" +
   743:     encodeURIComponent(String(date || "").trim());
   744: 
   745:   return "https://app.kingapostas.com/results/details?" + q;
   746: }
   747: 
   748: async function axiosGetText(url) {
   749:   let lastErr = null;
   750: 
   751:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= HTTP_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   752:     try {
   753:       const { data } = await axios.get(url, {
   754:         headers: {
   755:           Accept:
   756:             "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8",
   757:           Origin: "https://app.kingapostas.com",
   758:           Referer: "https://app.kingapostas.com/",
   759:           "User-Agent":
   760:             "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120 Safari/537.36",
   761:         },
   762:         timeout: 30000,
   763:         responseType: "text",
   764:         validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 400,
   765:       });
   766:       return String(data || "");
   767:     } catch (e) {
   768:       lastErr = e;
   769:       const canRetry = attempt < HTTP_RETRIES && shouldRetryAxiosError(e);
   770:       if (!canRetry) break;
   771: 
   772:       const backoff = HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, attempt);
   773:       console.warn(
   774:         `[HTTP] tentativa ${attempt + 1}/${HTTP_RETRIES + 1} (HTML) falhou (${
   775:           e?.response?.status || e?.code || "err"
   776:         }). retry em ${backoff}ms...`
   777:       );
   778:       await sleep(backoff);
   779:     }
   780:   }
   781: 
   782:   throw lastErr || new Error("Falha ao buscar HTML");
   783: }
   784: 
   785: /**
   786:  * Detecta se o HTML é uma página de login/redirect (evita falso positivo).
   787:  */
   788: function detectDetailsHtmlKind(html) {
   789:   const h = String(html || "");
   790:   const low = h.toLowerCase();
   791: 
   792:   // sinais comuns:
   793:   if (low.includes("/login/sign-in") || low.includes("formlogin")) return "login";
   794:   if (low.includes("bailout_to_client_side_rendering")) return "csr_bailout";
   795:   if (low.includes("app/login") || low.includes("sign-in")) return "login_like";
   796: 
   797:   // fallback: parece uma página de results
   798:   if (low.includes("results/details") || low.includes("kingapostas"))
   799:     return "results_like";
   800: 
   801:   return "unknown";
   802: }
   803: 
   804: // Heurística: só diagnosticar se o HTML "parece" ter o slot e algum conteúdo de prêmios
   805: function parseDetailsHtmlForSlot(html, slotHHMM) {
   806:   const out = {
   807:     hasSlot: false,
   808:     hasAnyPrize: false,
   809:     kind: "unknown",
   810:   };
   811:   const h = String(html || "");
   812:   const slot = String(slotHHMM || "").trim();
   813:   if (!h || !slot) return out;
   814: 
   815:   out.kind = detectDetailsHtmlKind(h);
   816: 
   817:   // Se é login/redirect, não confia em nada
   818:   if (out.kind === "login" || out.kind === "login_like") {
   819:     return out;
   820:   }
   821: 
   822:   if (h.toLowerCase().includes(slot.toLowerCase())) out.hasSlot = true;
   823: 
   824:   // mantém heurística, mas só vale quando não for login
   825:   if (/(prize[_\s-]?\d+)|(\b\d{4}\b)/i.test(h)) out.hasAnyPrize = true;
   826: 
   827:   return out;
   828: }
   829: 
   830: async function tryHtmlDetailsFallback({ date, lotteryKey, closeHour }) {
   831:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   832:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
   833:   if (!slot || !isHHMM(slot)) return { ok: false, reason: "bad_slot" };
   834: 
   835:   // por enquanto: só PT_RIO 18:00 (não altera outros horários)
   836:   if (!(lk === "PT_RIO" && slot === "18:00")) {
   837:     return { ok: false, reason: "not_applicable" };
   838:   }
   839: 
   840:   const url = buildDetailsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
   841:   const html = await axiosGetText(url);
   842:   const parsed = parseDetailsHtmlForSlot(html, slot);
   843: 
   844:   return {
   845:     ok: true,
   846:     url,
   847:     slot,
   848:     hasSlot: parsed.hasSlot,
   849:     hasAnyPrize: parsed.hasAnyPrize,
   850:     kind: parsed.kind,
   851:   };
   852: }
   853: 
   854: /**
   855:  * ✅ Merge + dedup de draws vindos de múltiplos requests
   856:  * Dedup key: date + close_hour(SLOT) + lottery_id (se existir)
   857:  */
   858: function mergeAndDedupDraws(arrays, lotteryKey) {
   859:   const map = new Map();
   860: 
   861:   for (const a of arrays) {
   862:     const list = Array.isArray(a) ? a : [];
   863:     for (const d of list) {
   864:       const date = String(d?.date || "").trim();
   865:       const { slot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(d, lotteryKey);
   866: 
   867:       const lid =
   868:         pickLotteryId(d) ||
   869:         String(d?.lottery_name || d?.name || "").trim() ||
   870:         "NA";
   871:       const key = `${date}__${slot}__${lid}`;
   872: 
   873:       if (!map.has(key)) {
   874:         map.set(key, d);
   875:       } else {
   876:         // se duplicado, preferir o que tiver mais prizes preenchidos
   877:         const prev = map.get(key);
   878:         const prevCount = countPrizesInDraw(prev);
   879:         const curCount = countPrizesInDraw(d);
   880:         if (curCount > prevCount) map.set(key, d);
   881:       }
   882:     }
   883:   }
   884: 
   885:   return Array.from(map.values());
   886: }
   887: 
   888: function countPrizesInDraw(draw) {
   889:   let c = 0;
   890:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
   891:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
   892:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
   893:     c++;
   894:   }
   895:   return c;
   896: }
   897: 
   898: function summarizeCloseHours(draws, lotteryKey) {
   899:   const set = new Set();
   900: 
   901:   for (const d of draws) {
   902:     const { slot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
   903:       d,
   904:       lotteryKey
   905:     );
   906: 
   907:     if (slot) {
   908:       set.add(slot);
   909:     }
   910:   }
   911: 
   912:   return Array.from(set).sort();
   913: }
   914: 
   915: /**
   916:  * ✅ Fetch robusto:
   917:  * - Por default faz 1 request por lotteryId e mescla (evita “cap”/consolidação do endpoint)
   918:  * - Se KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY=0 -> faz 1 request único (modo antigo)
   919:  */
   920: async function fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey }) {
   921:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   922:   const lotteries = LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk];
   923:   if (!lotteries?.length) {
   924:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY, lk)) {
   925:       throw new Error(
   926:         `${lk} sem lotteryIds. Defina KING_LOTTERIES_${lk}="uuid1,uuid2,..." para habilitar o fetch.`
   927:       );
   928:     }
   929: 
   930:     throw new Error(`lotteryKey inválida: ${lk}`);
   931:   }
   932: 
   933:   if (!FETCH_PER_LOTTERY) {
   934:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
   935:     return await axiosGetJson(url);
   936:   }
   937: 
   938:   // ✅ 1 request por lottery (sequencial: mais estável e respeita rate-limit)
   939:   const parts = [];
   940:   const errors = [];
   941: 
   942:   // helper local: extrai e normaliza closes vindos nesse UUID
   943:   const closeFromDraw = (d) =>
   944:     normalizeHHMM(d?.close_hour ?? d?.closeHour ?? d?.horario ?? d?.close ?? "");
   945: 
   946:   const summarizeCloseHoursPart = (list) => {
   947:     const set = new Set();
   948:     for (const d of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) {
   949:       const c = closeFromDraw(d);
   950:       if (c) set.add(c);
   951:     }
   952:     return Array.from(set).sort();
   953:   };
   954: 
   955:   for (const lotteryId of lotteries) {
   956:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk, lotteryId });
   957: 
   958:     try {
   959:       const data = await axiosGetJson(url);
   960: 
   961:       // ✅ carimba o UUID do request em cada draw (a API nem sempre retorna lottery_id)
   962:       if (data && Array.isArray(data.data)) {
   963:         data.data = data.data.map((d) => ({
   964:           ...d,
   965:           lottery_id: d?.lottery_id || lotteryId,
   966:           lotteryId: d?.lotteryId || lotteryId,
   967:         }));
   968:       }
   969:       const list = Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : [];
   970:       parts.push(list);
   971: 
   972:       if (KING_FETCH_DEBUG) {
   973:         const closesPart = summarizeCloseHoursPart(list);
   974:         console.log(
   975:           `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} draws=${list.length} close_hours=[${closesPart.join(
   976:             ", "
   977:           )}]`
   978:         );
   979:       }
   980:     } catch (e) {
   981:       const msg = e?.response?.status
   982:         ? `HTTP_${e.response.status}`
   983:         : e?.code || e?.message || "ERR";
   984: 
   985:       errors.push({ lotteryId, msg });
   986: 
   987:       console.warn(
   988:         `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} ERROR=${msg} (seguindo...)`
   989:       );
   990: 
   991:       // segue para não travar o dia inteiro por 1 UUID
   992:       continue;
   993:     }
   994:   }
   995: 
   996:   const merged = mergeAndDedupDraws(parts, lk);
   997:   const closes = summarizeCloseHours(merged, lk);
   998:   const errInfo =
   999:     KING_FETCH_DEBUG && errors.length
  1000:       ? ` errors=${errors.length} ids=[${errors.map((x) => x.lotteryId).join(",")}]`
  1001:       : "";
  1002: 
  1003:   console.log(
  1004:     `[FETCH] ${lk} ${date} per-lottery=${lotteries.length} -> merged_draws=${
  1005:       merged.length
  1006:     } close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]${errInfo}`
  1007:   );
  1008:   return { success: true, data: merged };
  1009: }
  1010: 
  1011: /**
  1012:  * Monta prizes do draw (até 15), já filtrando vazios.
  1013:  */
  1014: function extractPrizes(draw) {
  1015:   const prizes = [];
  1016:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
  1017:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
  1018:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
  1019:     prizes.push({ position: i, value: String(v) });
  1020:   }
  1021:   return prizes;
  1022: }
  1023: 
  1024: /**
  1025:  * Gera drawId e drawRef no mesmo padrão do seu projeto.
  1026:  * ✅ Agora usa close_hour SLOT no ID e guarda close_hour_raw (quando fizer sentido).
  1027:  */
  1028: function buildDrawRef({ draw, lotteryKey }) {
  1029:   const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
  1030: 
  1031:   const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
  1032:     draw,
  1033:     lotteryKey
  1034:   );
  1035: 
  1036:   const lotteryName = String(draw?.lottery_name || draw?.name || "SEM_NOME").trim();
  1037: 
  1038:   const lotteryIdFromDraw = pickLotteryId(draw);
  1039:   const lotteryIdPart = safeIdPart(lotteryIdFromDraw || lotteryName);
  1040: 
  1041:   // O ID deve usar sempre o slot oficial.
  1042:   // O horario bruto da fonte pode variar, por exemplo 19:53,
  1043:   // e permanece preservado apenas em close_hour_raw.
  1044:   const drawIdSlot = closeSlot;
  1045: 
  1046:   const drawId = `${safeIdPart(lotteryKey)}__${date}__${safeIdPart(
  1047:     drawIdSlot
  1048:   )}__${lotteryIdPart}`;
  1049:   const drawRef = db.collection("draws").doc(drawId);
  1050: 
  1051:   return {
  1052:     drawId,
  1053:     drawRef,
  1054:     date,
  1055:     closeRaw,
  1056:     closeSlot,
  1057:     lotteryName,
  1058:     lotteryIdFromDraw,
  1059:   };
  1060: }
  1061: 
  1062: /**
  1063:  * Prova forte de “já completo”:
  1064:  * - draw existe
  1065:  * - prizesCount > 0 (ou) existe ao menos 1 doc na subcoleção prizes
  1066:  */
  1067: async function checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef) {
  1068:   try {
  1069:     const dSnap = await drawRef.get();
  1070:     if (!dSnap.exists) return false;
  1071: 
  1072:     const data = dSnap.data() || {};
  1073:     if (Number(data.prizesCount) > 0) return true;
  1074: 
  1075:     // fallback: olha 1 doc de prizes
  1076:     const pSnap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").limit(1).get();
  1077:     return !pSnap.empty;
  1078:   } catch {
  1079:     return false;
  1080:   }
  1081: }
  1082: 
  1083: /**
  1084:  * ✅ Prova forte por SLOT (anti-índice composto)
  1085:  * - Query filtra SOMENTE por date
  1086:  * - close_hour + lottery_key/lotteryKey filtrados em memória
  1087:  *
  1088:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
  1089:  * - Normaliza o close_hour armazenado (pode existir legado "09:09")
  1090:  *   e compara por SLOT (HH:00), para não “perder” doc existente.
  1091:  */
  1092: async function checkSlotCompletion({ date, closeHour, lotteryKey }) {
  1093:   try {
  1094:     const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1095:     const targetSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
  1096:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1097: 
  1098:     // query mínima
  1099:     const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
  1100:     if (snap.empty) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1101: 
  1102:     const refs = [];
  1103:     snap.forEach((doc) => {
  1104:       const d = doc.data() || {};
  1105: 
  1106:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1107:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1108: 
  1109:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;
  1110: 
  1111:       // ✅ compara por SLOT normalizado (pega legado "09:09" como "09:00")
  1112:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;
  1113:       if (storedSlot !== targetSlot) return;
  1114: 
  1115:       refs.push(doc.ref);
  1116:     });
  1117: 
  1118:     if (!refs.length) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1119: 
  1120:     let complete = 0;
  1121:     for (const ref of refs) {
  1122:       const ok = await checkAlreadyComplete(ref);
  1123:       if (ok) complete += 1;
  1124:     }
  1125: 
  1126:     return { docs: refs.length, complete };
  1127:   } catch {
  1128:     return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1129:   }
  1130: }
  1131: 
  1132: /**
  1133:  * Importa payload para o Firestore.
  1134:  * closeHour (opcional): se informado, importa SOMENTE draws daquele horário (SLOT).
  1135:  *
  1136:  * Opts extras:
  1137:  * - skipIfAlreadyComplete (boolean): se true, e o draw do closeHour já estiver completo no FS,
  1138:  *   então NÃO escreve nada (evita ruído e falsos positivos).
  1139:  */
  1140: async function importFromPayload({
  1141:   payload,
  1142:   lotteryKey,
  1143:   closeHour = null,
  1144:   skipIfAlreadyComplete = false,
  1145: } = {}) {
  1146:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1147:   const uf = resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lk); // ✅ UF correta (ex.: RJ)
  1148: 
  1149:   let batch = db.batch();
  1150:   let ops = 0;
  1151: 
  1152:   let totalDrawsFromApi = 0;
  1153:   let totalDrawsMatchedClose = 0;
  1154:   let totalDrawsValid = 0;
  1155: 
  1156:   let totalDrawsUpserted = 0;
  1157:   let totalDrawsSaved = 0;
  1158: 
  1159:   let totalPrizesUpserted = 0;
  1160:   let totalPrizesSaved = 0;
  1161: 
  1162:   let skippedInvalid = 0;
  1163:   let skippedEmpty = 0;
  1164:   let skippedCloseHour = 0;
  1165:   let skippedAlreadyComplete = 0;
  1166: 
  1167:   // Provas úteis para o scheduler (quando closeHour é informado)
  1168:   const proof = {
  1169:     filterClose: null,
  1170: 
  1171:     apiHasPrizes: false,
  1172:     apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0, // draws válidos do closeHour com prizes
  1173:     targetDrawIds: [],
  1174:     inferredDate: null, // date do slot (inferido a partir do payload)
  1175: 
  1176:     expectedTargets: 0, // = max(1, apiReturnedTargetDraws)
  1177:     slotDocsFound: 0,
  1178:     alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1179:     alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1180:     alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1181: 
  1182:     targetWriteCount: 0,
  1183:     targetSavedCount: 0,
  1184:   };
  1185: 
  1186:   // ✅ filtro deve ser SLOT também
  1187:   const filterClose = closeHour
  1188:     ? normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour, lk).slot
  1189:     : null;
  1190:   if (filterClose && !isHHMM(filterClose)) {
  1191:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "closeHour" inválido. Use HH:MM.');
  1192:   }
  1193:   proof.filterClose = filterClose;
  1194: 
  1195:   const apiArr = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
  1196: 
  1197:   for (const draw of apiArr) {
  1198:     totalDrawsFromApi++;
  1199: 
  1200:     const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
  1201:     const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
  1202:       draw,
  1203:       lk
  1204:     );
  1205: 
  1206:     // filtro por horário (SLOT)
  1207:     if (filterClose) {
  1208:       if (closeSlot !== filterClose) {
  1209:         skippedCloseHour++;
  1210:         continue;
  1211:       }
  1212:     }
  1213: 
  1214:     totalDrawsMatchedClose++;
  1215: 
  1216:     // validações mínimas (slot precisa ser HH:MM)
  1217:     if (!isISODate(date) || !isHHMM(closeSlot)) {
  1218:       skippedInvalid++;
  1219:       continue;
  1220:     }
  1221: 
  1222:     // prizes existentes
  1223:     const prizes = extractPrizes(draw);
  1224: 
  1225:     // Se não tem prize nenhum, NÃO grava
  1226:     if (!prizes.length) {
  1227:       skippedEmpty++;
  1228:       continue;
  1229:     }
  1230: 
  1231:     totalDrawsValid++;
  1232: 
  1233:     if (filterClose) {
  1234:       proof.apiHasPrizes = true;
  1235:       proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws += 1;
  1236:       proof.inferredDate = proof.inferredDate || date;
  1237:     }
  1238: 
  1239:     const { drawId, drawRef, lotteryName, lotteryIdFromDraw } = buildDrawRef({
  1240:       draw,
  1241:       lotteryKey: lk,
  1242:     });
  1243: 
  1244:     if (filterClose) proof.targetDrawIds.push(drawId);
  1245: 
  1246:     // Em closeHour: checa “já completo” e, se configurado, pula escrita (por draw)
  1247:     if (filterClose) {
  1248:       const already = await checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef);
  1249:       if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && already) {
  1250:         skippedAlreadyComplete++;
  1251:         continue;
  1252:       }
  1253:     }
  1254: 
  1255:     // métrica "novo" (opcional — custa reads)
  1256:     let isNewDraw = false;
  1257:     if (CHECK_EXISTENCE) {
  1258:       try {
  1259:         const snap = await drawRef.get();
  1260:         isNewDraw = !snap.exists;
  1261:       } catch {
  1262:         isNewDraw = false;
  1263:       }
  1264:     }
  1265: 
  1266:     const ymd = date;
  1267: 
  1268:     batch.set(
  1269:       drawRef,
  1270:       {
  1271:         source: "kingapostas",
  1272: 
  1273:         uf: uf || null,
  1274:         lottery_key: lk,
  1275: 
  1276:         lottery_name: lotteryName,
  1277:         lottery_id: lotteryIdFromDraw || null,
  1278:         drawId,
  1279: 
  1280:         date,
  1281:         ymd,
  1282: 
  1283:         // ✅ campo de negócio (SLOT)
  1284:         close_hour: closeSlot,
  1285: 
  1286:         // ✅ compat: campos usados no front (evita undefined)
  1287:         hour: closeSlot ? String(closeSlot).slice(0, 2) : null,
  1288:         close: closeSlot || null,
  1289: 
  1290:         // ✅ guarda o que veio da API SOMENTE quando fizer sentido
  1291:         // (PT_RIO HH:09 vira "", então cai em null)
  1292:         close_hour_raw: closeRaw || null,
  1293: 
  1294:         prizesCount: prizes.length,
  1295:         importedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  1296:       },
  1297:       { merge: true }
  1298:     );
  1299:     ops++;
  1300:     totalDrawsUpserted++;
  1301:     if (isNewDraw) totalDrawsSaved++;
  1302: 
  1303:     if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
  1304: 
  1305:     for (const p of prizes) {
  1306:       const n = normalizePrize(p.value);
  1307:       const prizeId = `p${String(p.position).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  1308:       const prizeRef = drawRef.collection("prizes").doc(prizeId);
  1309: 
  1310:       let isNewPrize = false;
  1311:       if (CHECK_EXISTENCE) {
  1312:         try {
  1313:           const psnap = await prizeRef.get();
  1314:           isNewPrize = !psnap.exists;
  1315:         } catch {
  1316:           isNewPrize = false;
  1317:         }
  1318:       }
  1319: 
  1320:       batch.set(
  1321:         prizeRef,
  1322:         {
  1323:           position: p.position,
  1324:           ...n,
  1325:         },
  1326:         { merge: true }
  1327:       );
  1328:       ops++;
  1329:       totalPrizesUpserted++;
  1330:       if (isNewPrize) totalPrizesSaved++;
  1331: 
  1332:       if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
  1333: 
  1334:       if (ops >= 420) {
  1335:         await batch.commit();
  1336:         batch = db.batch();
  1337:         ops = 0;
  1338:       }
  1339:     }
  1340:   }
  1341: 
  1342:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
  1343: 
  1344:   // ✅ flags finais de complete (por SLOT via query anti-índice)
  1345:   if (proof.filterClose) {
  1346:     const slotDate = proof.inferredDate; // se API trouxe o slot, teremos date
  1347:     proof.expectedTargets = Math.max(1, Number(proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws || 0));
  1348: 
  1349:     if (slotDate) {
  1350:       const { docs, complete } = await checkSlotCompletion({
  1351:         date: slotDate,
  1352:         closeHour: proof.filterClose,
  1353:         lotteryKey: lk,
  1354:       });
  1355: 
  1356:       proof.slotDocsFound = docs;
  1357:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = complete;
  1358:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = complete > 0;
  1359:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = complete >= proof.expectedTargets;
  1360:     } else {
  1361:       proof.slotDocsFound = 0;
  1362:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = 0;
  1363:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = false;
  1364:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = false;
  1365:     }
  1366: 
  1367:     // savedCount “forte” para o scheduler:
  1368:     if (!proof.apiHasPrizes) {
  1369:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
  1370:     } else if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && proof.alreadyCompleteAll) {
  1371:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
  1372:     } else {
  1373:       proof.targetSavedCount = proof.targetWriteCount;
  1374:     }
  1375:   }
  1376: 
  1377:   console.log(
  1378:     `[IMPORT] API draws=${totalDrawsFromApi} | match_close=${totalDrawsMatchedClose} | validos=${totalDrawsValid}` +
  1379:       ` | draws_upserted=${totalDrawsUpserted} | draws_novos=${totalDrawsSaved}` +
  1380:       ` | prizes_upserted=${totalPrizesUpserted} | prizes_novos=${totalPrizesSaved}` +
  1381:       ` | skip_vazio=${skippedEmpty} | skip_invalido=${skippedInvalid} | skip_close=${skippedCloseHour}` +
  1382:       ` | skip_complete=${skippedAlreadyComplete}` +
  1383:       ` | CHECK_EXISTENCE=${CHECK_EXISTENCE ? "ON" : "OFF"}` +
  1384:       ` | uf=${uf || "NULL"} lottery_key=${lk}` +
  1385:       (proof.filterClose
  1386:         ? ` | expected=${proof.expectedTargets} complete=${proof.alreadyCompleteCount} slotDocs=${proof.slotDocsFound} apiTargets=${proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws}`
  1387:         : "")
  1388:   );
  1389: 
  1390:   return {
  1391:     totalDrawsFromApi,
  1392:     totalDrawsMatchedClose,
  1393:     totalDrawsValid,
  1394: 
  1395:     totalDrawsSaved,
  1396:     totalDrawsUpserted,
  1397: 
  1398:     totalPrizesSaved,
  1399:     totalPrizesUpserted,
  1400: 
  1401:     skippedEmpty,
  1402:     skippedInvalid,
  1403:     skippedCloseHour,
  1404:     skippedAlreadyComplete,
  1405: 
  1406:     proof,
  1407:   };
  1408: }
  1409: 
  1410: /**
  1411:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
  1412:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)
  1413:  * - Seguro: só apaga legado se existe UUID no slot
  1414:  */
  1415: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
  1416:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1417:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
  1418:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
  1419: 
  1420:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
  1421:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
  1422: 
  1423:   const mine = rows.filter(
  1424:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
  1425:   );
  1426: 
  1427:   const bySlot = new Map();
  1428:   for (const r of mine) {
  1429:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
  1430:     if (!slot) continue;
  1431:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
  1432:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
  1433:   }
  1434: 
  1435:   let deleted = 0;
  1436:   let slots = 0;
  1437:   let batch = db.batch();
  1438:   let ops = 0;
  1439: 
  1440:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
  1441:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
  1442:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
  1443: 
  1444:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
  1445:     if (!leg.length) continue;
  1446: 
  1447:     for (const d of leg) {
  1448:       batch.delete(d.ref);
  1449:       deleted += 1;
  1450:       ops += 1;
  1451:       if (ops >= 450) {
  1452:         await batch.commit();
  1453:         batch = db.batch();
  1454:         ops = 0;
  1455:       }
  1456:     }
  1457:     slots += 1;
  1458:   }
  1459: 
  1460:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
  1461: 
  1462:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
  1463: }
  1464: 
  1465: async function runImport({ date, lotteryKey = "PT_RIO", closeHour = null } = {}) {
  1466:   if (!date || !isISODate(date)) {
  1467:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "date" inválido. Use YYYY-MM-DD.');
  1468:   }
  1469: 
  1470:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1471:   if (!lk || !LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk]) {
  1472:     throw new Error(`Parâmetro "lotteryKey" inválida: ${lk}`);
  1473:   }
  1474: 
  1475:   // ✅ BLINDAGEM: nunca importar data futura (evita “sujar” FS por engano)
  1476:   const todayBR = todayYMDLocal();
  1477:   if (!ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE && isFutureISODate(date)) {
  1478:     const norm = normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lk);
  1479:     return {
  1480:       ok: true,
  1481:       lotteryKey: lk,
  1482:       date,
  1483:       closeHour: norm.slot || null,
  1484: 
  1485:       requestedCloseHour: norm.requested || null,
  1486: 
  1487:       blocked: true,
  1488:       blockedReason: "future_date",
  1489:       todayBR,
  1490: 
  1491:       captured: false,
  1492:       apiHasPrizes: null,
  1493:       alreadyCompleteAny: null,
  1494:       alreadyCompleteAll: null,
  1495:       expectedTargets: null,
  1496:       alreadyCompleteCount: null,
  1497:       slotDocsFound: null,
  1498:       apiReturnedTargetDraws: null,
  1499:       savedCount: null,
  1500:       writeCount: null,
  1501:       targetDrawIds: null,
  1502:       tookMs: 0,
  1503: 
  1504:       totalDrawsFromApi: 0,
  1505:       totalDrawsMatchedClose: 0,
  1506:       totalDrawsValid: 0,
  1507:       totalDrawsSaved: 0,
  1508:       totalDrawsUpserted: 0,
  1509:       totalPrizesSaved: 0,
  1510:       totalPrizesUpserted: 0,
  1511:       skippedEmpty: 0,
  1512:       skippedInvalid: 0,
  1513:       skippedCloseHour: 0,
  1514:       skippedAlreadyComplete: 0,
  1515:       proof: {
  1516:         filterClose: norm.slot || null,
  1517:         apiHasPrizes: false,
  1518:         apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0,
  1519:         targetDrawIds: [],
  1520:         inferredDate: null,
  1521:         expectedTargets: 0,
  1522:         slotDocsFound: 0,
  1523:         alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1524:         alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1525:         alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1526:         targetWriteCount: 0,
  1527:         targetSavedCount: 0,
  1528:       },
  1529:     };
  1530:   }
  1531: 
  1532:   // ✅ closeHour do scheduler: normaliza SEM log enganoso
  1533:   const norm = normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lk);
  1534:   if (norm.note === "invalid") {
  1535:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "closeHour" inválido. Use HH:MM.');
  1536:   }
  1537:   const normalizedClose = norm.slot || null;
  1538: 
  1539:   const startedAt = Date.now();
  1540: 
  1541:   // ✅ SOFT OVERRIDE: PT_RIO 18:00 fora do calendário (controlado por env)
  1542:   const allowSoftOverride18 =
  1543:     lk === "PT_RIO" &&
  1544:     normalizedClose === "18:00" &&
  1545:     String(process.env.PT_RIO_SOFT_OVERRIDE_18 || "1").trim() === "1";
  1546: 
  1547:   // ✅ GATE POR CALENDÁRIO (PT_RIO)
  1548:   if (normalizedClose && lk === "PT_RIO") {
  1549:     const cal = getPtRioSlotsByDate(date);
  1550:     const expected = new Set((cal.core || []).concat(cal.opcional || []));
  1551: 
  1552:     if (!expected.has(normalizedClose)) {
  1553:       if (allowSoftOverride18) {
  1554:         try {
  1555:           console.log(
  1556:             "[CAL-OVERRIDE] PT_RIO " +
  1557:               date +
  1558:               " slot=" +
  1559:               normalizedClose +
  1560:               " fora do calendário -> tentando mesmo assim (SOFT 18h override)"
  1561:           );
  1562:         } catch {}
  1563:       } else {
  1564:         const ms0 = Date.now() - startedAt;
  1565:         return {
  1566:           ok: true,
  1567:           lotteryKey: lk,
  1568:           date,
  1569:           closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1570:           requestedCloseHour: norm.requested || null,
  1571: 
  1572:           blocked: true,
  1573:           blockedReason: "no_draw_for_slot_calendar",
  1574:           todayBR,
  1575:           captured: false,
  1576:           apiHasPrizes: false,
  1577:           alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1578:           alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1579:           expectedTargets: 0,
  1580:           alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1581:           slotDocsFound: 0,
  1582:           apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0,
  1583:           savedCount: 0,
  1584:           writeCount: 0,
  1585:           targetDrawIds: [],
  1586:           tookMs: ms0,
  1587:           totalDrawsFromApi: 0,
  1588:           totalDrawsMatchedClose: 0,
  1589:           totalDrawsValid: 0,
  1590:           totalDrawsSaved: 0,
  1591:           totalDrawsUpserted: 0,
  1592:           totalPrizesSaved: 0,
  1593:           totalPrizesUpserted: 0,
  1594:           skippedEmpty: 0,
  1595:           skippedInvalid: 0,
  1596:           skippedCloseHour: 0,
  1597:           skippedAlreadyComplete: 0,
  1598:           proof: {
  1599:             filterClose: normalizedClose,
  1600:             apiHasPrizes: false,
  1601:             apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0,
  1602:             targetDrawIds: [],
  1603:             inferredDate: null,
  1604:             expectedTargets: 0,
  1605:             slotDocsFound: 0,
  1606:             alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1607:             alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1608:             alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1609:             targetWriteCount: 0,
  1610:             targetSavedCount: 0,
  1611:           },
  1612:         };
  1613:       }
  1614:     }
  1615:   }
  1616: 
  1617:   // ✅ Fetch API (JSON)
  1618:   let payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
  1619: 
  1620:   // ✅ Se pediram closeHour e ele NÃO existe no dia (close_hours do FETCH), retorna blocked/api_missing_slot
  1621:   if (normalizedClose) {
  1622:     const closes = summarizeCloseHours(
  1623:       Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [],
  1624:       lk
  1625:     );
  1626:     const hasTarget = closes.includes(normalizedClose);
  1627: 
  1628:     if (!hasTarget) {
  1629:       // diagnóstico: tenta fallback HTML apenas para logar
  1630:       try {
  1631:         const fb = await tryHtmlDetailsFallback({
  1632:           date,
  1633:           lotteryKey: lk,
  1634:           closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1635:         });
  1636: 
  1637:         if (fb.ok) {
  1638:           console.warn(
  1639:             "[FALLBACK:DETAILS] lk=" +
  1640:               lk +
  1641:               " date=" +
  1642:               date +
  1643:               " slot=" +
  1644:               fb.slot +
  1645:               " kind=" +
  1646:               fb.kind +
  1647:               " hasSlot=" +
  1648:               fb.hasSlot +
  1649:               " hasAnyPrize=" +
  1650:               fb.hasAnyPrize +
  1651:               " url=" +
  1652:               fb.url
  1653:           );
  1654:         }
  1655:       } catch (e) {
  1656:         console.warn("[FALLBACK:DETAILS] erro: " + String(e?.message || e || "unknown"));
  1657:       }
  1658: 
  1659:       const ms0 = Date.now() - startedAt;
  1660: 
  1661:       return {
  1662:         ok: true,
  1663:         lotteryKey: lk,
  1664:         date,
  1665:         closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1666:         requestedCloseHour: norm.requested || null,
  1667: 
  1668:         blocked: true,
  1669:         blockedReason: "api_missing_slot",
  1670:         todayBR,
  1671: 
  1672:         captured: false,
  1673:         apiHasPrizes: false,
  1674:         alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1675:         alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1676: 
  1677:         expectedTargets: 0,
  1678:         alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1679:         slotDocsFound: 0,
  1680:         apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0,
  1681: 
  1682:         savedCount: 0,
  1683:         writeCount: 0,
  1684:         targetDrawIds: [],
  1685: 
  1686:         tookMs: ms0,
  1687: 
  1688:         totalDrawsFromApi: Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data.length : 0,
  1689:         totalDrawsMatchedClose: 0,
  1690:         totalDrawsValid: 0,
  1691:         totalDrawsSaved: 0,
  1692:         totalDrawsUpserted: 0,
  1693:         totalPrizesSaved: 0,
  1694:         totalPrizesUpserted: 0,
  1695:         skippedEmpty: 0,
  1696:         skippedInvalid: 0,
  1697:         skippedCloseHour: 0,
  1698:         skippedAlreadyComplete: 0,
  1699:         proof: {
  1700:           filterClose: normalizedClose,
  1701:           apiHasPrizes: false,
  1702:           apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0,
  1703:           targetDrawIds: [],
  1704:           inferredDate: null,
  1705:           expectedTargets: 0,
  1706:           slotDocsFound: 0,
  1707:           alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1708:           alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1709:           alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1710:           targetWriteCount: 0,
  1711:           targetSavedCount: 0,
  1712:         },
  1713:       };
  1714:     }
  1715:   }
  1716: 
  1717:   const result = await importFromPayload({
  1718:     payload,
  1719:     lotteryKey: lk,
  1720:     closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1721:     skipIfAlreadyComplete: Boolean(normalizedClose), // scheduler: evita regravar
  1722:   });
  1723: 
  1724:   const ms = Date.now() - startedAt;
  1725: 
  1726:   const proof = result.proof || {};
  1727:   const apiHasPrizes = Boolean(proof.apiHasPrizes);
  1728: 
  1729:   const alreadyCompleteAnyReal = Boolean(proof.alreadyCompleteAny);
  1730:   const alreadyCompleteAll = Boolean(proof.alreadyCompleteAll);
  1731: 
  1732:   const alreadyCompleteAny = normalizedClose ? alreadyCompleteAnyReal : null;
  1733: 
  1734:   // ✅ Captured “verdadeiro” (modo closeHour):
  1735:   // - API liberou prizes para o horário, OU
  1736:   // - slot já está completo no Firestore
  1737:   const captured = normalizedClose
  1738:     ? apiHasPrizes || alreadyCompleteAnyReal
  1739:     : (result.totalDrawsValid || 0) > 0;
  1740: 
  1741:   const writeCount = Number.isFinite(Number(proof.targetWriteCount))
  1742:     ? Number(proof.targetWriteCount)
  1743:     : 0;
  1744: 
  1745:   const savedCount = Number.isFinite(Number(proof.targetSavedCount))
  1746:     ? Number(proof.targetSavedCount)
  1747:     : 0;
  1748: 
  1749:   const response = {
  1750:     ok: true,
  1751:     lotteryKey: lk,
  1752:     date,
  1753:     closeHour: normalizedClose || null,
  1754:     requestedCloseHour: norm.requested || null,
  1755: 
  1756:     blocked: false,
  1757:     blockedReason: null,
  1758:     todayBR,
  1759: 
  1760:     captured,
  1761:     apiHasPrizes: normalizedClose ? apiHasPrizes : null,
  1762:     alreadyCompleteAny: normalizedClose ? alreadyCompleteAny : null,
  1763:     alreadyCompleteAll: normalizedClose ? alreadyCompleteAll : null,
  1764: 
  1765:     expectedTargets: normalizedClose ? Number(proof.expectedTargets || 0) : null,
  1766:     alreadyCompleteCount: normalizedClose ? Number(proof.alreadyCompleteCount || 0) : null,
  1767:     slotDocsFound: normalizedClose ? Number(proof.slotDocsFound || 0) : null,
  1768:     apiReturnedTargetDraws: normalizedClose ? Number(proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws || 0) : null,
  1769: 
  1770:     savedCount: normalizedClose ? savedCount : null,
  1771:     writeCount: normalizedClose ? writeCount : null,
  1772:     targetDrawIds: normalizedClose ? proof.targetDrawIds || [] : null,
  1773: 
  1774:     tookMs: ms,
  1775:     ...result,
  1776:   };
  1777: 
  1778:   const top3HistorySync =
  1779:     await syncImportedResultToTop3History(
  1780:       response
  1781:     );
  1782: 
  1783:   let top3AutoPrediction = {
  1784:     ok: true,
  1785:     skipped: true,
  1786:     reason: "not_attempted",
  1787:   };
  1788: 
  1789:   if (
  1790:     response.ok === true &&
  1791:     response.blocked !== true &&
  1792:     response.captured === true &&
  1793:     normalizedClose
  1794:   ) {
  1795:     try {
  1796:       const publicApi = loadTop3PublicApi();
  1797: 
  1798:       const nextSlot =
  1799:         resolveNextTop3Slot({
  1800:           lotteryKey: lk,
  1801:           ymd: date,
  1802:           hour: normalizedClose,
  1803:           publicApi,
  1804:         });
  1805: 
  1806:       const nextYmd =
  1807:         String(nextSlot?.ymd || "").trim();
  1808: 
  1809:       const nextHour =
  1810:         String(nextSlot?.hour || "").trim();
  1811: 
  1812:       if (
  1813:         /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(nextYmd) &&
  1814:         nextHour
  1815:       ) {
  1816:         const generated =
  1817:           await createTop3PredictionRun({
  1818:             lotteryKey: lk,
  1819:             date: nextYmd,
  1820:             closeHour: nextHour,
  1821:             historySource: "auto",
  1822:             source: "auto-import",
  1823:             metadata: {
  1824:               triggeredByImport: true,
  1825:               importedYmd: date,
  1826:               importedHour: normalizedClose,
  1827:             },
  1828:           });
  1829: 
  1830:         top3AutoPrediction = {
  1831:           ok: true,
  1832:           skipped: false,
  1833:           targetYmd: nextYmd,
  1834:           targetHour: nextHour,
  1835:           runId:
  1836:             generated?.run?.id || null,
  1837:           publicProjection:
  1838:             generated?.publicProjection || null,
  1839:         };
  1840: 
  1841:         console.log(
  1842:           `[TOP3 AUTO] OK | ${lk} | ` +
  1843:           `importado=${date} ${normalizedClose} | ` +
  1844:           `próximo=${nextYmd} ${nextHour}`
  1845:         );
  1846:       } else {
  1847:         top3AutoPrediction = {
  1848:           ok: true,
  1849:           skipped: true,
  1850:           reason: "next_slot_not_found",
  1851:         };
  1852: 
  1853:         console.warn(
  1854:           `[TOP3 AUTO] próximo slot não localizado | ` +
  1855:           `${lk} ${date} ${normalizedClose}`
  1856:         );
  1857:       }
  1858:     } catch (error) {
  1859:       top3AutoPrediction = {
  1860:         ok: false,
  1861:         skipped: false,
  1862:         error: String(
  1863:           error?.message ||
  1864:           error ||
  1865:           "top3_auto_failed"
  1866:         ),
  1867:       };
  1868: 
  1869:       console.error(
  1870:         "[TOP3 AUTO] falhou:",
  1871:         error?.stack ||
  1872:         error?.message ||
  1873:         error
  1874:       );
  1875:     }
  1876:   } else {
  1877:     top3AutoPrediction = {
  1878:       ok: true,
  1879:       skipped: true,
  1880:       reason:
  1881:         response.blocked === true
  1882:           ? "import_blocked"
  1883:           : response.captured !== true
  1884:           ? "result_not_captured"
  1885:           : "close_hour_missing",
  1886:     };
  1887:   }
  1888: 
  1889:   return {
  1890:     ...response,
  1891:     top3HistorySync,
  1892:     top3AutoPrediction,
  1893:   };
  1894: }
  1895: 
  1896: /**
  1897:  * CLI wrapper (mantém compatibilidade)
  1898:  */
  1899: async function main() {
  1900:   const date = process.argv[2];
  1901:   const lotteryKey = process.argv[3] || "PT_RIO";
  1902:   const closeHour = process.argv[4] || null;
  1903: 
  1904:   if (!date || !isISODate(date)) {
  1905:     throw new Error(
  1906:       "Uso: node backend/scripts/importKingApostas.js YYYY-MM-DD [PT_RIO|FEDERAL|LOOK|NACIONAL] [HH:MM]"
  1907:     );
  1908:   }
  1909: 
  1910:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1911:   if (!lk || !LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk]) {
  1912:     throw new Error(
  1913:       "Uso: node backend/scripts/importKingApostas.js YYYY-MM-DD [PT_RIO|FEDERAL|LOOK|NACIONAL] [HH:MM]"
  1914:     );
  1915:   }
  1916: 
  1917:   // ✅ no CLI também bloqueia data futura (por segurança)
  1918:   const todayBR = todayYMDLocal();
  1919:   if (!ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE && isFutureISODate(date)) {
  1920:     console.error(
  1921:       `[BLOCK] future_date: date=${date} todayBR=${todayBR} (defina ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE=1 para liberar)`
  1922:     );
  1923:     process.exit(2);
  1924:   }
  1925: 
  1926:   const norm = normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lk);
  1927:   if (norm.note === "invalid") {
  1928:     throw new Error(
  1929:       "Uso: node backend/scripts/importKingApostas.js YYYY-MM-DD [PT_RIO|FEDERAL|LOOK|NACIONAL] [HH:MM]"
  1930:     );
  1931:   }
  1932:   const normalizedClose = norm.slot || null;
  1933: 
  1934:   // ✅ LOG HONESTO (mostra o que foi pedido e o slot efetivo)
  1935:   const closeInfo =
  1936:     norm.requested && normalizedClose
  1937:       ? norm.requested === normalizedClose
  1938:         ? ` ${normalizedClose}`
  1939:         : ` req=${norm.requested} -> slot=${normalizedClose}`
  1940:       : normalizedClose
  1941:       ? ` ${normalizedClose}`
  1942:       : norm.requested
  1943:       ? ` req=${norm.requested}`
  1944:       : "";
  1945: 
  1946:   console.log(`STEP 1/3 Buscando API: ${lk} ${date}${closeInfo}`);
  1947: 
  1948:   let payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
  1949: 
  1950:   // ✅ NOVO (CLI também): se pediram closeHour e ele não existe no dia, avisa e sai 0
  1951:   if (normalizedClose) {
  1952:     const closes = summarizeCloseHours(
  1953:       Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [],
  1954:       lk
  1955:     );
  1956:     const hasTarget = closes.includes(normalizedClose);
  1957: 
  1958:     if (!hasTarget) {
  1959:       console.warn(
  1960:         `[NO_DRAW_FOR_SLOT] ${lk} ${date} slot=${normalizedClose} close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]`
  1961:       );
  1962: 
  1963:       // tenta fallback apenas pra logar
  1964:       try {
  1965:         const fb = await tryHtmlDetailsFallback({
  1966:           date,
  1967:           lotteryKey: lk,
  1968:           closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1969:         });
  1970:         if (fb.ok) {
  1971:           console.warn(
  1972:             `[FALLBACK:DETAILS] lk=${lk} date=${date} slot=${fb.slot} kind=${fb.kind} hasSlot=${fb.hasSlot} hasAnyPrize=${fb.hasAnyPrize} url=${fb.url}`
  1973:           );
  1974:         }
  1975:       } catch {}
  1976: 
  1977:       process.exit(0);
  1978:     }
  1979:   }
  1980: 
  1981:   console.log(
  1982:     `STEP 2/3 Processando ${Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data.length : 0
  1983:     } draws retornados pela API...`
  1984:   );
  1985: 
  1986:   await importFromPayload({
  1987:     payload,
  1988:     lotteryKey: lk,
  1989:     closeHour: normalizedClose,
  1990:     skipIfAlreadyComplete: false, // CLI regrava
  1991:   });
  1992: 
  1993:   console.log(
  1994:     `STEP 3/3 OK. Import concluído: ${lk} ${date}${normalizedClose ? ` ${normalizedClose}` : ""
  1995:     }`
  1996:   );
  1997: 
  1998:   // ✅ cleanup automático de legado (somente quando per-lottery está ON)
  1999:   if (FETCH_PER_LOTTERY) {
  2000:     try {
  2001:       const c = await cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
  2002:       if (c && c.ok && c.deleted) {
  2003:         console.log(`[CLEANUP] ${lk} ${date} slots=${c.slots} deleted=${c.deleted}`);
  2004:       } else {
  2005:         console.log(`[CLEANUP] ${lk} ${date} slots=${c?.slots || 0} deleted=${c?.deleted || 0}`);
  2006:       }
  2007:     } catch (e) {
  2008:       console.warn(`[CLEANUP] ${lk} ${date} ERROR=${String(e?.message || e || "")}`);
  2009:     }
  2010:   }
  2011: }
  2012: 
  2013: if (require.main === module) {
  2014:   main().catch((e) => {
  2015:     console.error("ERRO:", e?.message || e);
  2016:     process.exit(1);
  2017:   });
  2018: }

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const fs = require("fs");
     4: const path = require("path");
     5: 
     6: // ✅ usa o bootstrap oficial do seu projeto (service account / config já pronta)
     7: const { admin, getDb } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
     8: 
     9: function arg(name, def = null) {
    10:   const i = process.argv.indexOf(`--${name}`);
    11:   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1]) return process.argv[i + 1];
    12:   return def;
    13: }
    14: 
    15: function isISODateStrict(s) {
    16:   const str = String(s ?? "").trim();
    17:   if (!str) return false;
    18:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
    19:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
    20:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    21:   return (
    22:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
    23:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
    24:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
    25:   );
    26: }
    27: 
    28: function normalizeLotteryKey(v, fallback = "") {
    29:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
    30:   if (!s) return fallback;
    31: 
    32:   if (s === "RJ" || s === "RIO" || s === "PT-RIO" || s === "PT_RIO") return "PT_RIO";
    33:   if (s === "FED" || s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
    34: 
    35:   return fallback;
    36: }
    37: 
    38: function pad2(n) {
    39:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
    40: }
    41: 
    42: function normHourWithStatus(v) {
    43:   const s0 = String(v ?? "").trim();
    44:   if (!s0) return { close: "", status: "EMPTY" };
    45: 
    46:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
    47: 
    48:   let m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
    49:   if (m) {
    50:     const hh = Number(m[1]);
    51:     if (Number.isFinite(hh) && hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) {
    52:       return { close: `${pad2(hh)}:00`, status: "OK" };
    53:     }
    54:     return { close: "", status: "INVALID" };
    55:   }
    56: 
    57:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
    58:   if (m) {
    59:     const hh = Number(m[1]);
    60:     const mm = Number(m[2]);
    61:     if (
    62:       Number.isFinite(hh) &&
    63:       Number.isFinite(mm) &&
    64:       hh >= 0 &&
    65:       hh <= 23 &&
    66:       mm >= 0 &&
    67:       mm <= 59
    68:     ) {
    69:       return { close: `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}`, status: "OK" };
    70:     }
    71:     return { close: "", status: "INVALID" };
    72:   }
    73: 
    74:   m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
    75:   if (m) {
    76:     const hh = Number(m[1]);
    77:     if (Number.isFinite(hh) && hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) {
    78:       return { close: `${pad2(hh)}:00`, status: "OK" };
    79:     }
    80:     return { close: "", status: "INVALID" };
    81:   }
    82: 
    83:   return { close: "", status: "INVALID" };
    84: }
    85: 
    86: function ymdInRange(ymd, from, to) {
    87:   if (!ymd) return false;
    88:   if (from && ymd < from) return false;
    89:   if (to && ymd > to) return false;
    90:   return true;
    91: }
    92: 
    93: function safeFilePart(s) {
    94:   return String(s ?? "")
    95:     .trim()
    96:     .replace(/[^\w.\-]+/g, "_")
    97:     .slice(0, 120);
    98: }
    99: 
   100: function resolveLogsDir() {
   101:   const cwd = process.cwd();
   102:   const base = path.basename(cwd).toLowerCase();
   103:   if (base === "backend") return path.join(cwd, "logs");
   104:   return path.join(cwd, "backend", "logs");
   105: }
   106: 
   107: (async () => {
   108:   try {
   109:     const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(arg("lottery", "PT_RIO"), "PT_RIO");
   110:     const from = arg("from", null);
   111:     const to = arg("to", null);
   112: 
   113:     if (from && !isISODateStrict(from)) throw new Error(`--from inválido (use YYYY-MM-DD): ${from}`);
   114:     if (to && !isISODateStrict(to)) throw new Error(`--to inválido (use YYYY-MM-DD): ${to}`);
   115:     if (from && to && from > to) throw new Error(`intervalo inválido: from (${from}) > to (${to})`);
   116: 
   117:     const db = getDb();
   118:     const col = db.collection("draws");
   119: 
   120:     let scanned = 0;
   121:     let lastYmd = null;
   122:     let lastId = null;
   123: 
   124:     const buckets = new Map();
   125: 
   126:     while (true) {
   127:       let q = col
   128:         .orderBy("ymd")
   129:         .orderBy(admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId())
   130:         .limit(500);
   131: 
   132:       if (lastYmd !== null && lastId !== null) q = q.startAfter(lastYmd, lastId);
   133: 
   134:       const snap = await q.get();
   135:       if (snap.empty) break;
   136: 
   137:       for (const doc of snap.docs) {
   138:         scanned++;
   139:         const d = doc.data() || {};
   140: 
   141:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").slice(0, 10);
   142:         if (!ymdInRange(ymd, from, to)) continue;
   143: 
   144:         const lk = normalizeLotteryKey(d.lottery_key || d.lotteryKey || d.lottery || "", "");
   145:         if (lk && lk !== lottery) continue;
   146: 
   147:         const { close, status: closeNormStatus } = normHourWithStatus(
   148:           d.close_hour || d.closeHour || d.hour || d.hora || ""
   149:         );
   150: 
   151:         const key = `${lk || lottery}__${ymd}__${close || "??"}`;
   152: 
   153:         if (!buckets.has(key)) buckets.set(key, []);
   154:         buckets.get(key).push({
   155:           id: doc.id,
   156:           ymd,
   157:           lottery_key: lk || lottery,
   158:           close_hour: d.close_hour ?? d.closeHour ?? null,
   159:           close_hour_raw: d.close_hour_raw ?? d.closeHourRaw ?? null,
   160:           close_norm: close || null,
   161:           close_norm_status: closeNormStatus,
   162:           prizesCount: d.prizesCount ?? (Array.isArray(d.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : null),
   163:           importedAt: d.importedAt ?? null,
   164:           source: d.source ?? null,
   165:         });
   166:       }
   167: 
   168:       const lastDoc = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
   169:       lastYmd = String((lastDoc.data() || {}).ymd || "");
   170:       lastId = lastDoc.id;
   171: 
   172:       if (snap.size < 500) break;
   173:     }
   174: 
   175:     const dups = [];
   176:     for (const [key, arr] of buckets.entries()) {
   177:       if (arr.length > 1) {
   178:         arr.sort((a, b) => String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id)));
   179:         dups.push({ key, count: arr.length, docs: arr });
   180:       }
   181:     }
   182: 
   183:     dups.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.key.localeCompare(b.key));
   184: 
   185:     const outDir = resolveLogsDir();
   186:     if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
   187: 
   188:     const outFile = path.join(
   189:       outDir,
   190:       `dups-${safeFilePart(lottery)}-${safeFilePart(from || "ALL")}_to_${safeFilePart(to || "ALL")}.json`
   191:     );
   192: 
   193:     fs.writeFileSync(
   194:       outFile,
   195:       JSON.stringify({ lottery, from, to, scanned, duplicates: dups }, null, 2),
   196:       "utf8"
   197:     );
   198: 
   199:     console.log("OK ✅");
   200:     console.log("Scanned docs:", scanned);
   201:     console.log("Duplicate keys:", dups.length);
   202:     console.log("Saved:", outFile);
   203: 
   204:     for (const x of dups.slice(0, 30)) {
   205:       console.log(`\nDUP: ${x.key} (x${x.count})`);
   206:       x.docs.forEach((d) =>
   207:         console.log(
   208:           " -",
   209:           d.id,
   210:           "| close:",
   211:           d.close_hour,
   212:           "| norm:",
   213:           d.close_norm,
   214:           "| status:",
   215:           d.close_norm_status,
   216:           "| raw:",
   217:           d.close_hour_raw,
   218:           "| prizes:",
   219:           d.prizesCount,
   220:           "| importedAt:",
   221:           d.importedAt
   222:         )
   223:       );
   224:     }
   225:   } catch (e) {
   226:     console.error("ERRO:", e?.stack || e?.message || e);
   227:     process.exit(1);
   228:   }
   229: })();

     1: import { doc, getDoc, serverTimestamp, setDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
     2: import { db } from "./firebase";
     3: import { getKingResultsByRange } from "./kingResultsService";
     4: import { buildRanking } from "../utils/buildRanking";
     5: import { applyScoreEngine } from "../utils/scoreEngine";
     6: 
     7: function safeArray(v) {
     8:   return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
     9: }
    10: 
    11: function normalizeUfKey(uf) {
    12:   return String(uf || "PT_RIO").trim().toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    13: }
    14: 
    15: function snapshotDocRef(uf, scope = "dashboard_full") {
    16:   const key = normalizeUfKey(uf);
    17:   return doc(db, "statistics", key, "snapshots", scope);
    18: }
    19: 
    20: export async function readStatisticsSnapshot({ uf, scope = "dashboard_full" } = {}) {
    21:   const ref = snapshotDocRef(uf, scope);
    22:   const snap = await getDoc(ref);
    23: 
    24:   if (!snap.exists()) {
    25:     return {
    26:       exists: false,
    27:       data: null,
    28:     };
    29:   }
    30: 
    31:   return {
    32:     exists: true,
    33:     data: snap.data(),
    34:   };
    35: }
    36: 
    37: export async function buildStatisticsSnapshot({
    38:   uf = "PT_RIO",
    39:   dateFrom,
    40:   dateTo,
    41:   scope = "dashboard_full",
    42:   positions = null,
    43: } = {}) {
    44:   if (!dateFrom || !dateTo) {
    45:     throw new Error("buildStatisticsSnapshot exige dateFrom e dateTo.");
    46:   }
    47: 
    48:   const draws = await getKingResultsByRange({
    49:     uf,
    50:     dateFrom,
    51:     dateTo,
    52:     positions,
    53:     mode: "detailed",
    54:   });
    55: 
    56:   const built = buildRanking(draws);
    57:   const ranking = safeArray(built?.ranking);
    58:   const rankingScored = applyScoreEngine(ranking);
    59: 
    60:   const payload = {
    61:     version: 1,
    62:     scope,
    63:     uf: normalizeUfKey(uf),
    64:     from: dateFrom,
    65:     to: dateTo,
    66:     totalDraws: Number(built?.totalDraws || 0),
    67:     uniqueDays: Number(built?.uniqueDays || 0),
    68:     totalOcorrencias: Number(built?.totalOcorrencias || 0),
    69:     ranking,
    70:     rankingScored,
    71:     top3: safeArray(rankingScored).slice(0, 3),
    72:     integrityIssues: safeArray(built?.integrityIssues),
    73:     countMode: built?.countMode || "byPrize",
    74:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    75:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    76:   };
    77: 
    78:   await setDoc(snapshotDocRef(uf, scope), payload, { merge: true });
    79: 
    80:   return payload;
    81: }
    82: 
    83: export async function ensureStatisticsSnapshot({
    84:   uf = "PT_RIO",
    85:   dateFrom,
    86:   dateTo,
    87:   scope = "dashboard_full",
    88:   force = false,
    89:   positions = null,
    90: } = {}) {
    91:   const current = await readStatisticsSnapshot({ uf, scope });
    92: 
    93:   if (!force && current.exists && current.data?.from === dateFrom && current.data?.to === dateTo) {
    94:     return {
    95:       source: "cache",
    96:       data: current.data,
    97:     };
    98:   }
    99: 
   100:   const data = await buildStatisticsSnapshot({
   101:     uf,
   102:     dateFrom,
   103:     dateTo,
   104:     scope,
   105:     positions,
   106:   });
   107: 
   108:   return {
   109:     source: "rebuilt",
   110:     data,
   111:   };
   112: }

     1: name: Auto Import — PalPitaco JB

     3: on:
     4:   # Automatic scheduling migrated to Google Cloud Scheduler.

     8:       lottery:
     9:         description: "Qual loteria executar"
    10:         required: true
    11:         default: "ALL"
    12:         type: choice

    14:           - ALL
    15:           - PT_RIO
    16:           - FEDERAL
    17:           - LOOK
    18:           - NACIONAL

    21: # Cada execução reconcilia todas as loterias e recupera slots anteriores.

    23:   group: auto-import-heartbeat-${{ github.ref }}
    24:   cancel-in-progress: false
    25:

    27:   contents: read
    28:

    30:   TZ: America/Sao_Paulo

    35:     name: Reconciliar resultados
    36:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    37:     timeout-minutes: 30
    38: 
    39:     defaults:
    40:       run:
    41:         working-directory: backend
    42: 
    43:     steps:
    44:       - name: Checkout do repositório
    45:         uses: actions/checkout@v4
    46: 
    47:       - name: Setup Node.js
    48:         uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    49:         with:
    50:           node-version: 20
    51:           cache: npm
    52:           cache-dependency-path: backend/package-lock.json
    53: 
    54:       - name: Instalar dependências
    55:         run: npm ci
    56:

    58:         shell: bash
    59:         env:
    60:           FIREBASE_ADMIN_SA_JSON: ${{ secrets.FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON }}
    61:           FAIL_ON_CRITICAL: "1"
    62:           AUDIT_WARN_MIN: "20"
    63:           AUDIT_CRIT_MIN: "60"
    64:           CATCHUP_MAX_AFTER_END_MIN: "1440"
    65:         run: |
    66:           set +e
    67: 
    68:           requested="${{ inputs.lottery }}"
    69: 
    70:           if [[ "${{ github.event_name }}" == "workflow_dispatch" && "$requested" != "ALL" ]]; then
    71:             lotteries="$requested"
    72:           else
    73:             lotteries="PT_RIO FEDERAL LOOK NACIONAL"
    74:           fi
    75: 
    76:           yesterday="$(TZ=America/Sao_Paulo date -d 'yesterday' +%F)"
    77:           overall=0
    78: 
    79:           run_import() {
    80:             local lottery="$1"
    81:             local period="$2"
    82:             shift 2
    83: 
    84:             echo ""
    85:             echo "=================================================="
    86:             echo "LOTTERY=$lottery | PERIOD=$period"
    87:             echo "=================================================="
    88: 
    89:             env LOTTERY="$lottery" "$@" node scripts/autoImportToday.js
    90:             local code=$?
    91: 
    92:             if [[ $code -ne 0 ]]; then
    93:               echo "::error::Falha LOTTERY=$lottery PERIOD=$period EXIT=$code"
    94:               overall=1
    95:             fi
    96:           }
    97: 
    98:           for lottery in $lotteries; do
    99:             run_import "$lottery" "TODAY"
   100:           done
   101: 
   102:           for lottery in $lotteries; do
   103:             run_import \
   104:               "$lottery" \
   105:               "YESTERDAY:$yesterday" \
   106:               DATE="$yesterday" \
   107:               NOW_HM="23:59"
   108:           done
   109: 
   110:           exit $overall
